":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"40dbd08c-0bc6-48f2-bc0c-66789ad825a1","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"mcp_instructions_delta","addedNames":["plugin:context7:context7","plugin:serena:serena"],"addedBlocks":["## plugin:context7:context7\nUse this server to fetch current documentation whenever the user asks about a library, framework, SDK, API, CLI tool, or cloud service -- even well-known ones like React, Next.js, Prisma, Express, Tailwind, Django, or Spring Boot. This includes API syntax, configuration, version migration, library-specific debugging, setup instructions, and CLI tool usage. Use even when you think you know the answer -- your training data may not reflect recent changes. Prefer this over web search for library docs.\n\nDo not use for: refactoring, writing scripts from scratch, debugging business logic, code review, or general programming concepts.","## plugin:serena:serena\nCRITICAL: Before starting to work on a coding task, call the `initial_instructions` tool to read the 'Serena Instructions Manual'."],"removedNames":[]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"f8099f84-a789-40a3-a0a0-752bcbd43332","timestamp":"2026-04-30T14:58:17.742Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"f314b500-bade-49f3-91a7-f08ec7c91411","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"f8099f84-a789-40a3-a0a0-752bcbd43332","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"skill_listing","content":"- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring or one-time remote agent (\"run this every Monday\", \"open a cleanup PR for X in 2 weeks\"), or to manage existing routines. ALSO OFFER PROACTIVELY: after you finish work that has a natural future follow-up, end your reply with a one-line offer to schedule a background agent to do it. Strong signals: a feature flag / gate / experiment / staged rollout was just shipped (offer a one-time agent in ~2 weeks to open a cleanup PR or evaluate results), a new alert/monitor was created (offer a recurring agent to triage it), a TODO/migration with a \"remove once X\" condition was left behind (offer a one-time agent to do the removal). Skip the offer for refactors, bug fixes, and anything that is done once it ships. Name a concrete action and cadence (\"in 2 weeks\", \"every Monday\") and only offer when the run just succeeded — do not pitch a schedule for something that has not happened yet.\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- ccs-delegation: Auto-activate CCS CLI delegation for deterministic tasks. Parses user input, auto-selects optimal profile (glm/kimi/custom) from ~/.ccs/config.json, enhances prompts with context, executes via `ccs {profile} -p \"task\"` or `ccs {profile}:continue`, and reports results. Triggers on \"use ccs [task]\" patterns, typo/test/refactor keywords. Excludes complex architecture, security-critical code, performance optimization, breaking changes.\n- attach: Port the Claude Code harness layer from this project to a new project directory. Use when bootstrapping a fresh Claude Code project on the same machine, when peers want to copy the harness, or when verifying layer separation via dry-run. Reads .claude/manifest.yaml; copies harness-tagged artifacts only; excludes stockforge biz + personal layers; generates skeleton CLAUDE.md + manifest at target.\n- crawler-reliability: Build reliable web crawlers for data gathering. Use when implementing scrapers for CafeF, NDH, VietnamBiz, YouTube (yt-dlp), Facebook fanpages, or any external data source. Covers Python/Playwright patterns, retry/backoff, selector robustness, rate limiting, monitoring.\n- ddd-tactical-patterns: Apply DDD tactical patterns (aggregate, entity, value object, repository, domain event, anti-corruption layer) correctly in Python/dataclasses code. Use when designing or implementing domain models, repositories, or cross-bounded-context communication in StockForge's 9 BCs.\n- decompose-work: Split a non-trivial task into deterministic-vs-LLM portions before execution. Use when planning multi-step work where some sub-tasks are mechanical (file ops, regex, count, schema validation) and others require judgment (synthesis, design, ambiguity resolution). Outputs structured plan that maps each portion to the cheapest competent tool. Pairs with capability-map.md (model × effort × task_class grounding) and try-n-approaches (S9 — Karpathy autoresearch loop).\n- evidence-extraction: Extract structured claims from unstructured sources with citation integrity. Use when building extraction pipelines, parsing news articles or KOL transcripts, or anywhere claims are created from raw data. Ensures every claim has source_url, extracted_at, confidence, and verified attribution. Enforces no-LLM-math invariant (I-S1).\n- fastapi-module: Implement FastAPI routers following StockForge conventions. Use when creating a new HTTP endpoint, adding routes, wiring dependency injection, configuring Pydantic DTOs. Covers router boundaries, provider patterns, testing setup. Phase 2+ only — Phase 1 uses Streamlit dashboard directly.\n- grill-maximization: Doctrine for bundling Q&A questions to the human at maximum density per turn (15-20 target, max 25), so each human-in-the-loop touchpoint is information-rich. Use when composing a Q&A bundle for human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, or when deciding whether to ask one question or wait and bundle. Built on the principle that human-in-the-loop time is the scarcest resource in autonomous coding.\n- hook-diagnostics: Inspect hook event lifecycle (state machine + transcript cache + aggregator output) when triaging failed/stuck hooks or auditing self-awareness rollups. Use during sandwich-verifier review, drift investigation, or post-mortem when component-telemetry.jsonl + dispatch.jsonl + sessions-rollup.tsv need cross-checking.\n- obsidian-vault: Search, create, and manage notes in the StockForge Obsidian vault with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index maintenance. Use when adding content to the knowledge base, navigating entities/concepts, or maintaining the raw/wiki separation. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed. Extended with raw/wiki pattern from Karpathy LLM Wiki.\n- postgres-pgvector: Design Postgres schemas with pgvector and TimescaleDB for StockForge. Use when creating migrations, designing tables, writing queries. Covers vector embeddings for semantic search on Vietnamese text, time-series for price/sentiment data, JSONB vs structured columns.\n- promote-rule: Cluster agent-notes.md entries by lexical similarity, propose promotion of recurring rules to deterministic hook (priority 1), skill (priority 2), or charter (priority 3). Use when agent-notes has accumulated ≥10 new rules since last promotion run, or on-demand at phase boundary. Outputs proposal markdown to observations/. Pairs with capability-map.md (writes new task_class entries) and decompose-work (consumes promotion outcomes).\n- prompt-engineering: Design and optimize LLM prompts for StockForge pipeline stages. Use when writing or refining prompts for claim extraction, KOL recommendation extraction, sentiment analysis, thesis synthesis. Covers context assembly, prompt caching strategy, budget-aware prompts, structured output. Enforces no-LLM-math constraint.\n- qa-escalation: File-based Q&A protocol — composes bundle file in human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/, tracks lifecycle (pending → answered → stale → processed), creates URGENT notification when needed. Use when intent classifier recommends OPEN_QA_BUNDLE / ESCALATE_HUMAN, or grill-maximization has a bundle ready to write. Pairs with grill-maximization (composes content) + Track 5 hooks (auto-move stale).\n- session-memory-l0-l1: Extract structured memories (L0 quick metadata + L1 deep) from a Claude Code session JSONL transcript. L0 is pure regex (file paths, commands, errors, failures incl. Vietnamese phrases, next step). L1 ships the prompt builder + response parser + 15+35 head/tail windowing — caller dispatches the LLM (no auto-invoke in Phase 0). Plus cleanText harness-stripper + TranscriptCache (mtime+size dual-check + LRU). Use when ingesting a session for memory promotion, computing what files/commands/failures the session touched, or preparing an offline LLM extraction.\n- spec-dual-layer: Write specifications with dual-layer structure (business narrative + agent contract). Use when authoring new specs, refining existing specs, or understanding the spec template. Ensures both human stakeholder needs and agent execution needs are served.\n- spec-to-wiki: Convert raw specifications to Obsidian wiki format with wikilinks, frontmatter, and index updates. Use when adding a new spec to the knowledge base, or when regenerating wiki view after spec changes. Maintains raw as source of truth, wiki as navigable view.\n- sync-pull: On-demand confidence-score lookup before a SCOPE+/ARCH/IMPL decision. Reads agent-workspace/memory/sync-tracker/state.tsv + weights.yaml; surfaces the relevant category's score, tier, must-grill flag, and the decision-class threshold. Output drives \"self-decide vs grill\" choice. Use whenever an agent is about to commit a non-trivial decision and needs to ground confidence in historical hit rate per Charter Principle 8 (Calibration over confidence).\n- test-pyramid-balance: Balance unit, integration, and E2E tests properly in Python/pytest. Use when deciding what test to write, reviewing test coverage, or rebalancing skewed test suite. Covers test pyramid shape, BDD vs unit tests, when to use what. Includes stock-domain-specific test categories.\n- try-n-approaches: Karpathy autoresearch outer-loop framing — given a task or signal, generate ≥3 distinct approaches (DEEPEN / BROADEN / ABANDON families), pick one per cheap-first ordering, attach a deterministic metric function (per L-S12-1), and write the experiment frame to learning-data/loop/. Use when an open question surfaces from drift / dogfood / sync-grilling and you need to commit a measurable next step. Pairs with decompose-work (deterministic-vs-LLM split per approach) + capability-map (model × effort grounding) + promote-rule (consumes outcomes).\n- ubiquitous-language: Extract a DDD-style ubiquitous language glossary from the current conversation or domain area. Use when starting work on a new bounded context, when new domain terms emerge, or when verifying consistency between business language and code. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed.\n- user-prompt-intake: Hybrid intent classifier — main session lite-detects trivial prompts, dispatches intent-classifier subagent for complex ones. Use whenever a new user prompt lands (chat or file in human-workspace/user_prompt/) and you need to route the work. Built for full-autonomous loop where every prompt must be classified before action.\n- write-a-skill: Create new Claude Code skills with proper structure, progressive disclosure, and discoverability. Use when extracting a pattern from multiple sessions into a reusable skill, or when user asks to formalize a technique. Adapted from mattpocock/skills, MIT licensed.\n- ccs:continue: Continue last CCS delegation session [AUTO ENHANCE]\n- ccs: Delegate task with intelligent profile selection [AUTO ENHANCE]\n- budget-check: /budget-check — Current Session Token Budget Status\n- devils-advocate: /devils-advocate — Adversarial Critique\n- drift-check: /drift-check — Run Drift Signals DR1-DR12\n- drill-me: /drill-me — Interactive Ubiquitous Language Extraction\n- grill-me: /grill-me — Relentless Plan/Design Interview\n- handoff-read: /handoff-read — Lightweight Last Session Load\n- master-plan: /master-plan — High-Level Work Decomposition\n- session-end: /session-end — Close Work Session\n- session-start: /session-start — Begin Work Session\n- session-verify: /session-verify — Mid-Session Alignment Check\n- spec-author: /spec-author — Create Dual-Layer Specification\n- spec-to-wiki: /spec-to-wiki — Convert Raw Spec to Obsidian Wiki Format\n- ul-audit: /ul-audit — Audit Ubiquitous Language Consistency\n- vbw-check: /vbw-check — Apply Verify-Before-Write Protocol\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch","skillCount":49,"isInitial":true},"type":"attachment","uuid":"f041e2e8-1b87-499d-94e2-45d188d9667b","timestamp":"2026-04-30T14:58:17.742Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"f314b500-bade-49f3-91a7-f08ec7c91411","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"78063d8a-dfe7-49a1-9a28-542ded913595","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"nested_memory","path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","content":{"path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md","type":"Project","content":"# agent-workspace/ — Contract\n\n> **Audience**: agents working inside `agent-workspace/`.\n> **Established**: Decision 002 § Track 1 (Workspace Dualism Foundation).\n\n## Identity\n\n`agent-workspace/` is the **agent-owned execution + memory** layer. Everything here represents **agent state, decisions, plans, observations, learning**. Agents WRITE here freely (within constitution rules); humans READ here but normally do not edit.\n\nThis separation exists to prevent two failure modes from orch:\n1. **Charter drift via shared-workspace mutation** (orch CF-DOGFOOD-2)\n2. **Human cognitive overload from dense session logs** (UP02 §1.3 — \"human đối mặt hàng chục spec/document quá tải nếu không sắp xếp khoa học\")\n\nThe agent owns this directory but must keep it AUDITABLE — every artifact provenance-linked, every decision sourced.\n\n---\n\n## Subdirectories\n\n| Path | Purpose | Lifecycle |\n|---|---|---|\n| `constitution/` | **IMMUTABLE without explicit user approval**. Charter-tier rules (autonomous-protocol, model-routing, identity-scope, etc.). Track 7 produces drafts in `proposals/`; user explicit approve to move here. | Edit denied by .claude/settings.json; Write only via human approval gate |\n| `memory/project.md` | High-level project state; phase tracker; ADRs (last 5). Update on phase boundaries + architectural decisions. | Long-lived; trim to most recent decisions |\n| `memory/current-execution.md` | **THE single routing source of truth**. Active phase + active session + autonomous_mode flag + routing table. Read FIRST every session. | Long-lived; updated each session boundary |\n| `memory/agent-notes.md` | Learned rules from real experience (post-mortem / drift / user correction). Append-only mostly. | Long-lived |\n| `memory/decisions/` | Sequential ADRs (`NNN-<slug>.md`) + `_template.md` + `README.md` index. 12-field schema (Track 2). | Append-only; supersession via status field |\n| `memory/sessions/` | One file per session: `YYYY-MM-DD-session-N.md`. What happened, decisions made, files touched. | Append-only |\n| `memory/observations/` | Subagent return artifacts (per Track 6 spec). Each subagent writes one observation file describing what it did. | Append; cleaned periodically by aggregator |\n| `memory/checkpoints/` | Session handoff state for self-reboot (Track 5). `latest.md` is canonical pointer; timestamped historical files retained. | Append + latest.md updated each checkpoint |\n| `memory/patterns-discovered/` | Pattern mining outputs (Track 0 — DONE) + ongoing pattern catalog. | Append; significant patterns promoted to constitution |\n| `memory/drift-logs/` | Drift-check (DR1-DR12) results; runs on hook + on-demand. | Time-series append |\n| `memory/post-mortems/` | After significant failure or thesis-revoked event: what failed, root cause, prevention rule. | Append |\n| `memory/thesis-log/` | Stock-domain thesis exploration entries. Read-only for IMPL sessions. | Append + revisited per calibration |\n| `memory/sync-tracker/` | Track 8a output: Confidence Score SQLite + weights.yaml + auto-rendered `_index.md`. | Live updated by hooks |\n| `memory/self-awareness/` | Track 9 output: model × effort × thinking profile cards + known-issues + best-practices. | Live updated by Stop-hook aggregator |\n| `memory/mistake-log.md` | Track 7 deliverable: structured failure catalog. What went wrong / Root cause / Prevention rule / Severity. | Append; pre-flight read by all agents |\n| `session-plans/pending/` | Plans authored, awaiting execution. Phase 0 plan lives here. | Move to `completed/` when done |\n| `session-plans/completed/` | Executed plans; archived for reference + retrospective input. | Append |\n| `quality-reports/{deterministic,probabilistic,drift-reports}/` | Tier-1/2/3 gate outputs (CLAUDE.md § Quality Gates). | Per-run files |\n| `ubiquitous-language/` | DDD glossary + domain-mapping + drift-log. Stockforge stock-domain vocabulary. | Append; canonical via `/drill-me` |\n| `calibration/` | Empirical hit-rate data per signal type. Confidence claims MUST trace here (CLAUDE.md hard rule). | Append per thesis post-mortem |\n| `research/` | Research notes from `research-scanner` subagent dispatches (e.g., reference-repo studies). | Append |\n\n---\n\n## Contract Rules (BINDING)\n\n1. **Constitution is immutable absent explicit human approval.** `Edit/Write` to `agent-workspace/constitution/**` is in `.claude/settings.json` deny list. Track 7 writes constitution drafts to `proposals/`; user explicit approve moves them.\n\n2. **`memory/decisions/` is append-mostly + supersession-status.** Don't delete decisions; mark `status: superseded-by-D-NNN` to revoke. Sequential numbering (`001`, `002`, ...) — don't reuse numbers.\n\n3. **`current-execution.md` is the routing source-of-truth.** No skill, agent, or command may hardcode phase paths or session paths from memory; resolve via this file. Stale paths in CLAUDE.md or skills = anti-pattern.\n\n4. **Never edit `obsidian-vault/raw/`.** Immutable. Vault writes only to `wiki/`. (Established in stockforge CLAUDE.md.)\n\n5. **Provenance is mandatory.** Every decision file references its source (human prompt / spec clause / drift detection / agent inference). Every thesis cites source + as-of date (I-S2). Every confidence claim cites historical hit rate (I-S20-equivalent).\n\n6. **Agents never `git commit` in this directory unless user explicitly requests.** Stage only.\n\n7. **`patterns-discovered/` artifacts are READ-ONLY for production code.** Patterns must be promoted to constitution / skills / hook scripts before becoming binding. (Otherwise they're informal notes, not contracts.)\n\n---\n\n## Reading Priority for Agent\n\nPer `agent-workspace/constitution/autonomous-protocol.md` (Track 7) and stockforge `CLAUDE.md` § Session Protocol:\n\n1. **`memory/current-execution.md`** — first; resolve active track\n2. **`memory/project.md`** — project state\n3. **`memory/checkpoints/latest.md`** if a recent checkpoint exists (within last 24h) — resume context\n4. **`memory/agent-notes.md`** — learned rules\n5. **`memory/mistake-log.md`** — pre-flight failure catalog\n6. **Last 3 files in `memory/sessions/`** — recent context\n7. **`session-plans/pending/<active-plan>.md`** — current plan\n8. **Relevant constitution files** as task demands (don't load all every session)\n9. **Relevant skills + agents + commands** as task demands\n\n---\n\n## Anti-Patterns to Avoid\n\n(From `memory/patterns-discovered/SYNTHESIS.md` § 6, full catalog 23 items. Highest-priority for this directory:)\n\n- **AP-1 Same-agent self-review** — never review your own implementation; dispatch fresh-context verifier\n- **AP-2 Self-track wind-down** — `.transcript-tokens` real-transcript is authoritative, not LLM self-track\n- **AP-5 Charter-coherence defer overriding user-CRITICAL** — re-read all `user_prompt/*` at every phase entry; never silently defer USER-CRITICAL\n- **AP-7 Performative SC ticking** — defer with explicit prerequisites > fire vacuous proposals\n- **AP-8 Pre-staged work causing checkpoint drift** — update `current-execution.md` immediately on task complete, not session-end\n- **AP-17 Identity drift** — stockforge is AI-first VN stock advisory; not generic financial framework (see `constitution/identity-scope.md` once Track 7 ships)\n- **AP-23 Continuous LLM-Guardian** — deterministic hooks = Guardian; LLM Guardian only at session-end aggregation\n\n---\n\n## Connection to human-workspace/\n\nThis directory is the agent's domain. Human's domain is `human-workspace/`. The two communicate through:\n- Human → Agent: `human-workspace/user_prompt/`, `human-workspace/decisions/`\n- Agent → Human: `human-workspace/notifications/`, `human-workspace/q-and-a/pending/`\n- Bidirectional: `human-workspace/q-and-a/answered/` (agent writes pending; human moves answered)\n- Audit trail: this directory's `memory/decisions/` references `human-workspace/` source files via path pointers\n\nAgent never writes to `human-workspace/` outside the designated `q-and-a/pending/` channel.\n","contentDiffersFromDisk":false},"displayPath":"agent-workspace\\CLAUDE.md"},"type":"attachment","uuid":"71f30632-25f9-4d86-9e28-742145a4f6e4","timestamp":"2026-04-30T14:58:25.614Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"f314b500-bade-49f3-91a7-f08ec7c91411","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

— Tier 1 thin-slice ingestion driver for VHM (Phase 1 exemplar).\n\nOrchestrates VnstockAdapter (VCI) + SsiAdapter (iBoard) via\nReconciliationService, persists the union of source Bars via\nSqliteBarRepository, and emits a reconciliation report markdown.\n\nPer S32 Track A R2 closure (D-012): TCBS public REST endpoints returned 404\nacross all probed variants; SSI iBoard chart-history is the primary alternate\nsource paired with VCI for Rule 4 multi-source reconciliation. TcbsAdapter\nremains wired in graceful-fail mode for archive symmetry — invoked but\nexpected to raise; reconciliation simply ignores its empty output.\n\nRun: `python apps/cli/ingest_vhm.py --start 2025-04-30 --end 2026-04-29`.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sys\nfrom datetime import date, timedelta\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport click\n\nfrom packages.contracts import Ticker\nfrom packages.infrastructure.market_data import (\n    ReconciledBar,\n    ReconciliationConfidence,\n    ReconciliationService,\n    SqliteBarRepository,\n    SsiAdapter,\n    SsiApiError,\n    TcbsAdapter,\n    TcbsApiError,\n    VnstockAdapter,\n    VnstockAdapterError,\n)\n\n\ndef _ensure_utf8_stdout() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Reconfigure stdout to UTF-8 so vnstock's Vietnamese banner does not\n    UnicodeEncodeError under Windows cp1252. Called from `main` before the\n    first VnstockAdapter construction (which lazily imports vnstock).\n    \"\"\"\n    _reconfigure = getattr(sys.stdout, \"reconfigure\", None)\n    if callable(_reconfigure):\n        _reconfigure(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n\n\n@click.command()\n@click.option(\"--ticker\", default=\"VHM\", show_default=True)\n@click.option(\"--start\", default=None, help=\"ISO date; default = end - 365 days\")\n@click.option(\"--end\", default=None, help=\"ISO date; default = today\")\n@click.option(\n    \"--output\",\n    default=\"./data/vhm.sqlite\",\n    show_default=True,\n    type=click.Path(dir_okay=False, path_type=Path),\n)\n@click.option(\"--report\", default=None, type=click.Path(dir_okay=False, path_type=Path))\ndef main(ticker: str, start: str | None, end: str | None, output: Path, report: Path | None) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Ingest a year of VHM (default) DAILY bars; reconcile vnstock vs TCBS.\"\"\"\n    _ensure_utf8_stdout()\n    end_date = date.fromisoformat(end) if end else date.today()\n    start_date = date.fromisoformat(start) if start else end_date - timedelta(days=365)\n    report_path = report if report is not None else output.with_name(output.stem + \"-reconciliation.md\")\n    t = Ticker(ticker)\n\n    click.echo(f\"[ingest_vhm] ticker={t.symbol} window={start_date}..{end_date}\")\n    click.echo(f\"[ingest_vhm] output={output} report={report_path}\")\n\n    vnstock_adapter = VnstockAdapter()\n    ssi_adapter = SsiAdapter()\n    tcbs_adapter = TcbsAdapter()\n    reconciler = ReconciliationService()\n    repo = SqliteBarRepository(db_path=output)\n\n    try:\n        vnstock_bars = vnstock_adapter.fetch_daily(t, start_date, end_date)\n        click.echo(f\"[ingest_vhm] vnstock returned {len(vnstock_bars)} bars\")\n    except VnstockAdapterError as exc:\n        click.echo(f\"[ingest_vhm] vnstock FAILED: {exc}\", err=True)\n        return 2\n\n    try:\n        ssi_bars = ssi_adapter.fetch_daily(t, start_date, end_date)\n        click.echo(f\"[ingest_vhm] ssi returned {len(ssi_bars)} bars\")\n    except SsiApiError as exc:\n        click.echo(f\"[ingest_vhm] ssi unavailable: {exc}\", err=True)\n        ssi_bars = []\n\n    try:\n        tcbs_bars = tcbs_adapter.fetch_daily(t, start_date, end_date)\n        click.echo(f\"[ingest_vhm] tcbs returned {len(tcbs_bars)} bars\")\n    except TcbsApiError as exc:\n        click.echo(f\"[ingest_vhm] tcbs unavailable (expected per D-012): {exc}\", err=True)\n        tcbs_bars = []\n\n    written = repo.save_many(list(vnstock_bars) + list(ssi_bars) + list(tcbs_bars))\n    click.echo(f\"[ingest_vhm] wrote {written} rows; total in db = {repo.count()}\")\n\n    reconciled = reconciler.reconcile(vnstock_bars, ssi_bars)\n    _write_report(report_path, t, start_date, end_date, reconciled)\n    click.echo(f\"[ingest_vhm] reconciliation report written: {report_path}\")\n    click.echo(\"[ingest_vhm] OK\")\n    return 0\n\n\ndef _write_report(\n    path: Path,\n    ticker: Ticker,\n    start: date,\n    end: date,\n    rows: list[ReconciledBar],\n) -> None:\n    high = sum(1 for r in rows if r.confidence == ReconciliationConfidence.HIGH)\n    low = sum(1 for r in rows if r.confidence == ReconciliationConfidence.LOW)\n    single = sum(1 for r in rows if r.confidence == ReconciliationConfidence.SINGLE_SOURCE)\n    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    lines: list[str] = [\n        f\"# Reconciliation Report — {ticker.symbol}\",\n        \"\",\n        f\"**Window**: {start} → {end}\",\n        f\"**Total rows**: {len(rows)}\",\n        f\"- HIGH (within 1%): {high}\",\n        f\"- LOW (>1% divergence): {low}\",\n        f\"- SINGLE_SOURCE: {single}\",\n        \"\",\n        \"## Sample divergences (LOW)\",\n        \"\",\n    ]\n    samples = [r for r in rows if r.confidence == ReconciliationConfidence.LOW][:5]\n    if not samples:\n        lines.append(\"(none)\")\n    else:\n        for r in samples:\n            closes = {k.value: str(v.amount) for k, v in r.close_by_source.items()}\n            lines.append(f\"- {r.period_end}: divergence={r.divergence_pct} closes={closes}\")\n    path.write_text(\"\\n\".join(lines) + \"\\n\", encoding=\"utf-8\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main(standalone_mode=False) or 0)\n","numLines":143,"startLine":1,"totalLines":143}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"0f4a1692-2d92-4a06-b969-c11c13040be9","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"f314b500-bade-49f3-91a7-f08ec7c91411","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"cb9b2b23-8acf-4f3f-8480-917fddf63c0e","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"3ce0f5ce-cede-4075-b864-0f5a79e07f65","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01FhC3ft29kpLpYnxcovT1CZ","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"ReconciliationService — pure-logic cross-source Bar reconciliation per Rule 4.\n2\t\n3\tTakes Bar lists from two providers (VNSTOCK + TCBS in the S28 thin slice) and\n4\temits a `ReconciledBar` view per (ticker, period_end) key. No LLM, no network,\n5\tno DB — pure deterministic comparison logic exercising financial-data-protocol\n6\tRule 4 (\"Source Attribution & Reconciliation\").\n7\t\n8\tConfidence rules (matches Rule 4 verbatim):\n9\t- Both sources present, divergence ≤ 1%: confidence = HIGH; reconciled close\n10\t  = arithmetic mean of the two close values.\n11\t- Both sources present, divergence > 1%: confidence = LOW; reconciled close\n12\t  = primary (vnstock) close; both source values surfaced for audit.\n13\t- Only one source has the day: confidence = SINGLE_SOURCE; reconciled close\n14\t  = whatever was available; the missing source is recorded.\n15\t\n16\tManual override path (Rule 4 final clause: \"Manual override always wins, with\n17\toverride_reason documented\") is deferred to Phase 2 — Phase 1 thin-slice has\n18\tno human-in-the-loop reconciliation override per Q-S28-3.\n19\t\n20\tDivergence formula: `abs(close_a - close_b) / max(close_a, close_b)`. Currency\n21\tmust match across the two close values; cross-currency comparison raises\n22\t`CurrencyMismatchError` (re-raised from Money operators per Rule 5).\n23\t\"\"\"\n24\t\n25\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n26\t\n27\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n28\tfrom datetime import date\n29\tfrom decimal import Decimal\n30\tfrom enum import StrEnum\n31\t\n32\tfrom packages.contracts import CurrencyMismatchError, Money, SourceProvider, Ticker\n33\tfrom packages.domain.market_data.models import Bar\n34\t\n35\t__all__ = [\n36\t    \"ReconciledBar\",\n37\t    \"ReconciliationConfidence\",\n38\t    \"ReconciliationService\",\n39\t]\n40\t\n41\t\n42\t_DEFAULT_TOLERANCE = Decimal(\"0.01\")  # 1% per Rule 4\n43\t\n44\t\n45\tclass ReconciliationConfidence(StrEnum):\n46\t    \"\"\"Confidence taxonomy for a reconciled price point.\"\"\"\n47\t\n48\t    HIGH = \"high\"\n49\t    LOW = \"low\"\n50\t    SINGLE_SOURCE = \"single_source\"\n51\t\n52\t\n53\t@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\n54\tclass ReconciledBar:\n55\t    \"\"\"View row over two source Bars at the same (ticker, period_end) key.\"\"\"\n56\t\n57\t    ticker: Ticker\n58\t    period_end: date\n59\t    close_reconciled: Money\n60\t    confidence: ReconciliationConfidence\n61\t    divergence_pct: Decimal\n62\t    sources_present: tuple[SourceProvider, ...]\n63\t    close_by_source: dict[SourceProvider, Money]\n64\t\n65\t\n66\t@dataclass\n67\tclass ReconciliationService:\n68\t    \"\"\"Stateless cross-source Bar reconciler. Construct once, call `reconcile`.\"\"\"\n69\t\n70\t    tolerance: Decimal = _DEFAULT_TOLERANCE\n71\t\n72\t    def reconcile(\n73\t        self,\n74\t        primary: list[Bar],\n75\t        secondary: list[Bar],\n76\t    ) -> list[ReconciledBar]:\n77\t        \"\"\"Emit one ReconciledBar per unique (ticker, period_end) across inputs.\n78\t\n79\t        Inputs are not assumed sorted; the service builds (ticker, period_end)\n80\t        index per source and walks the union of keys ascending by period_end\n81\t        within each ticker. `primary` is treated as the leading provider\n82\t        (vnstock in S28); `secondary` is the cross-check (TCBS in S28).\n83\t        \"\"\"\n84\t        primary_index = self._index(primary)\n85\t        secondary_index = self._index(secondary)\n86\t        all_keys = sorted(set(primary_index.keys()) | set(secondary_index.keys()))\n87\t\n88\t        out: list[ReconciledBar] = []\n89\t        for key in all_keys:\n90\t            ticker, period_end = key\n91\t            p = primary_index.get(key)\n92\t            s = secondary_index.get(key)\n93\t            out.append(self._reconcile_single(ticker, period_end, p, s))\n94\t        return out\n95\t\n96\t    def _reconcile_single(\n97\t        self,\n98\t        ticker: Ticker,\n99\t        period_end: date,\n100\t        primary_bar: Bar | None,\n101\t        secondary_bar: Bar | None,\n102\t    ) -> ReconciledBar:\n103\t        if primary_bar is not None and secondary_bar is not None:\n104\t            divergence = self._divergence_pct(primary_bar.close, secondary_bar.close)\n105\t            if divergence <= self.tolerance:\n106\t                close_reconciled = self._mean(primary_bar.close, secondary_bar.close)\n107\t                confidence = ReconciliationConfidence.HIGH\n108\t            else:\n109\t                close_reconciled = primary_bar.close\n110\t                confidence = ReconciliationConfidence.LOW\n111\t            return ReconciledBar(\n112\t                ticker=ticker,\n113\t                period_end=period_end,\n114\t                close_reconciled=close_reconciled,\n115\t                confidence=confidence,\n116\t                divergence_pct=divergence,\n117\t                sources_present=(primary_bar.source_provider, secondary_bar.source_provider),\n118\t                close_by_source={\n119\t                    primary_bar.source_provider: primary_bar.close,\n120\t                    secondary_bar.source_provider: secondary_bar.close,\n121\t                },\n122\t            )\n123\t        present = primary_bar or secondary_bar\n124\t        assert present is not None  # one must be set per all_keys construction\n125\t        return ReconciledBar(\n126\t            ticker=ticker,\n127\t            period_end=period_end,\n128\t            close_reconciled=present.close,\n129\t            confidence=ReconciliationConfidence.SINGLE_SOURCE,\n130\t            divergence_pct=Decimal(\"0\"),\n131\t            sources_present=(present.source_provider,),\n132\t            close_by_source={present.source_provider: present.close},\n133\t        )\n134\t\n135\t    @staticmethod\n136\t    def _divergence_pct(a: Money, b: Money) -> Decimal:\n137\t        if a.currency != b.currency:\n138\t            raise CurrencyMismatchError(a.currency, b.currency)\n139\t        max_abs = max(a.amount, b.amount)\n140\t        if max_abs == 0:\n141\t            return Decimal(\"0\")\n142\t        return abs(a.amount - b.amount) / max_abs\n143\t\n144\t    @staticmethod\n145\t    def _mean(a: Money, b: Money) -> Money:\n146\t        if a.currency != b.currency:\n147\t            raise CurrencyMismatchError(a.currency, b.currency)\n148\t        return Money((a.amount + b.amount) / Decimal(\"2\"), a.currency)\n149\t\n150\t    @staticmethod\n151\t    def _index(bars: list[Bar]) -> dict[tuple[Ticker, date], Bar]:\n152\t        return {(b.ticker, b.period_end): b for b in bars}\n153\t"}]},"uuid":"a8b0f574-54c0-44c1-860d-629db31fde7e","timestamp":"2026-04-30T14:59:41.119Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\packages\\infrastructure\\market_data\\reconciliation_service.py","content":"\"\"\"ReconciliationService — pure-logic cross-source Bar reconciliation per Rule 4.\n\nTakes Bar lists from two providers (VNSTOCK + TCBS in the S28 thin slice) and\nemits a `ReconciledBar` view per (ticker, period_end) key. No LLM, no network,\nno DB — pure deterministic comparison logic exercising financial-data-protocol\nRule 4 (\"Source Attribution & Reconciliation\").\n\nConfidence rules (matches Rule 4 verbatim):\n- Both sources present, divergence ≤ 1%: confidence = HIGH; reconciled close\n  = arithmetic mean of the two close values.\n- Both sources present, divergence > 1%: confidence = LOW; reconciled close\n  = primary (vnstock) close; both source values surfaced for audit.\n- Only one source has the day: confidence = SINGLE_SOURCE; reconciled close\n  = whatever was available; the missing source is recorded.\n\nManual override path (Rule 4 final clause: \"Manual override always wins, with\noverride_reason documented\") is deferred to Phase 2 — Phase 1 thin-slice has\nno human-in-the-loop reconciliation override per Q-S28-3.\n\nDivergence formula: `abs(close_a - close_b) / max(close_a, close_b)`. Currency\nmust match across the two close values; cross-currency comparison raises\n`CurrencyMismatchError` (re-raised from Money operators per Rule 5).\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom datetime import date\nfrom decimal import Decimal\nfrom enum import StrEnum\n\nfrom packages.contracts import CurrencyMismatchError, Money, SourceProvider, Ticker\nfrom packages.domain.market_data.models import Bar\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"ReconciledBar\",\n    \"ReconciliationConfidence\",\n    \"ReconciliationService\",\n]\n\n\n_DEFAULT_TOLERANCE = Decimal(\"0.01\")  # 1% per Rule 4\n\n\nclass ReconciliationConfidence(StrEnum):\n    \"\"\"Confidence taxonomy for a reconciled price point.\"\"\"\n\n    HIGH = \"high\"\n    LOW = \"low\"\n    SINGLE_SOURCE = \"single_source\"\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\nclass ReconciledBar:\n    \"\"\"View row over two source Bars at the same (ticker, period_end) key.\"\"\"\n\n    ticker: Ticker\n    period_end: date\n    close_reconciled: Money\n    confidence: ReconciliationConfidence\n    divergence_pct: Decimal\n    sources_present: tuple[SourceProvider, ...]\n    close_by_source: dict[SourceProvider, Money]\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ReconciliationService:\n    \"\"\"Stateless cross-source Bar reconciler. Construct once, call `reconcile`.\"\"\"\n\n    tolerance: Decimal = _DEFAULT_TOLERANCE\n\n    def reconcile(\n        self,\n        primary: list[Bar],\n        secondary: list[Bar],\n    ) -> list[ReconciledBar]:\n        \"\"\"Emit one ReconciledBar per unique (ticker, period_end) across inputs.\n\n        Inputs are not assumed sorted; the service builds (ticker, period_end)\n        index per source and walks the union of keys ascending by period_end\n        within each ticker. `primary` is treated as the leading provider\n        (vnstock in S28); `secondary` is the cross-check (TCBS in S28).\n        \"\"\"\n        primary_index = self._index(primary)\n        secondary_index = self._index(secondary)\n        all_keys = sorted(set(primary_index.keys()) | set(secondary_index.keys()))\n\n        out: list[ReconciledBar] = []\n        for key in all_keys:\n            ticker, period_end = key\n            p = primary_index.get(key)\n            s = secondary_index.get(key)\n            out.append(self._reconcile_single(ticker, period_end, p, s))\n        return out\n\n    def _reconcile_single(\n        self,\n        ticker: Ticker,\n        period_end: date,\n        primary_bar: Bar | None,\n        secondary_bar: Bar | None,\n    ) -> ReconciledBar:\n        if primary_bar is not None and secondary_bar is not None:\n            divergence = self._divergence_pct(primary_bar.close, secondary_bar.close)\n            if divergence <= self.tolerance:\n                close_reconciled = self._mean(primary_bar.close, secondary_bar.close)\n                confidence = ReconciliationConfidence.HIGH\n            else:\n                close_reconciled = primary_bar.close\n                confidence = ReconciliationConfidence.LOW\n            return ReconciledBar(\n                ticker=ticker,\n                period_end=period_end,\n                close_reconciled=close_reconciled,\n                confidence=confidence,\n                divergence_pct=divergence,\n                sources_present=(primary_bar.source_provider, secondary_bar.source_provider),\n                close_by_source={\n                    primary_bar.source_provider: primary_bar.close,\n                    secondary_bar.source_provider: secondary_bar.close,\n                },\n            )\n        present = primary_bar or secondary_bar\n        assert present is not None  # one must be set per all_keys construction\n        return ReconciledBar(\n            ticker=ticker,\n            period_end=period_end,\n            close_reconciled=present.close,\n            confidence=ReconciliationConfidence.SINGLE_SOURCE,\n            divergence_pct=Decimal(\"0\"),\n            sources_present=(present.source_provider,),\n            close_by_source={present.source_provider: present.close},\n        )\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _divergence_pct(a: Money, b: Money) -> Decimal:\n        if a.currency != b.currency:\n            raise CurrencyMismatchError(a.currency, b.currency)\n        max_abs = max(a.amount, b.amount)\n        if max_abs == 0:\n            return Decimal(\"0\")\n        return abs(a.amount - b.amount) / max_abs\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _mean(a: Money, b: Money) -> Money:\n        if a.currency != b.currency:\n            raise CurrencyMismatchError(a.currency, b.currency)\n        return Money((a.amount + b.amount) / Decimal(\"2\"), a.currency)\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _index(bars: list[Bar]) -> dict[tuple[Ticker, date], Bar]:\n        return {(b.ticker, b.period_end): b for b in bars}\n","numLines":153,"startLine":1,"totalLines":153}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"cb9b2b23-8acf-4f3f-8480-917fddf63c0e","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"f314b500-bade-49f3-91a7-f08ec7c91411","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

Implements `BarRepository` Protocol.\"\"\"\n84\t\n85\t    db_path: Path\n86\t\n87\t    def __post_init__(self) -> None:\n88\t        self.db_path = Path(self.db_path)\n89\t        self.db_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n90\t        with closing(sqlite3.connect(str(self.db_path))) as conn:\n91\t            conn.executescript(_SCHEMA)\n92\t            conn.commit()\n93\t\n94\t    def save_many(self, bars: Iterable[Bar]) -> int:\n95\t        rows = [self._to_row(b) for b in bars]\n96\t        if not rows:\n97\t            return 0\n98\t        with closing(sqlite3.connect(str(self.db_path))) as conn:\n99\t            conn.executemany(\n100\t                \"\"\"\n101\t                INSERT OR REPLACE INTO bars (\n102\t                    bar_id, ticker, period_end, filing_date, ingested_at,\n103\t                    open_amount, high_amount, low_amount, close_amount, currency,\n104\t                    volume, foreign_buy, foreign_sell,\n105\t                    adjustment_type, source_provider\n106\t                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n107\t                \"\"\",\n108\t                rows,\n109\t            )\n110\t            conn.commit()\n111\t        return len(rows)\n112\t\n113\t    def get_as_of(self, ticker: Ticker, as_of_date: date) -> list[Bar]:\n114\t        sql = \"\"\"\n115\t            SELECT * FROM bars\n116\t            WHERE ticker = ?\n117\t              AND period_end <= ?\n118\t              AND filing_date <= ?\n119\t            ORDER BY period_end ASC\n120\t        \"\"\"\n121\t        return self._dedupe(self._query(sql, (ticker.symbol, as_of_date.isoformat(), as_of_date.isoformat())))\n122\t\n123\t    def get_latest(self, ticker: Ticker) -> Bar | None:\n124\t        bars = self._dedupe(\n125\t            self._query(\n126\t                \"SELECT * FROM bars WHERE ticker = ? ORDER BY period_end DESC LIMIT 10\",\n127\t                (ticker.symbol,),\n128\t            )\n129\t        )\n130\t        return bars[-1] if bars else None\n131\t\n132\t    def get_range_adjusted(\n133\t        self, ticker: Ticker, start: date, end: date\n134\t    ) -> list[Bar]:\n135\t        sql = \"\"\"\n136\t            SELECT * FROM bars\n137\t            WHERE ticker = ?\n138\t              AND period_end BETWEEN ? AND ?\n139\t              AND adjustment_type = ?\n140\t            ORDER BY period_end ASC\n141\t        \"\"\"\n142\t        return self._dedupe(\n143\t            self._query(sql, (ticker.symbol, start.isoformat(), end.isoformat(), AdjustmentType.BOTH.value))\n144\t        )\n145\t\n146\t    def count(self) -> int:\n147\t        with closing(sqlite3.connect(str(self.db_path))) as conn:\n148\t            cur = conn.execute(\"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM bars\")\n149\t            return int(cur.fetchone()[0])\n150\t\n151\t    def _query(self, sql: str, params: tuple[object, ...]) -> list[Bar]:\n152\t        with closing(sqlite3.connect(str(self.db_path))) as conn:\n153\t            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n154\t            cur = conn.execute(sql, params)\n155\t            return [self._from_row(dict(r)) for r in cur.fetchall()]\n156\t\n157\t    @staticmethod\n158\t    def _dedupe(bars: list[Bar]) -> list[Bar]:\n159\t        by_period: dict[date, Bar] = {}\n160\t        priority = {sp: i for i, sp in enumerate(_SOURCE_PRIORITY)}\n161\t        for b in bars:\n162\t            existing = by_period.get(b.period_end)\n163\t            if existing is None or priority.get(b.source_provider, 999) < priority.get(existing.source_provider, 999):\n164\t                by_period[b.period_end] = b\n165\t        return sorted(by_period.values(), key=lambda x: x.period_end)\n166\t\n167\t    @staticmethod\n168\t    def _to_row(b: Bar) -> tuple[object, ...]:\n169\t        return (\n170\t            b.bar_id,\n171\t            b.ticker.symbol,\n172\t — local SQLite Bar persistence implementing BarRepository.\n\nPhase 1 thin-slice substrate per spec 000 § A.5. TimescaleDB hypertable\ndeferred to Phase 2 when VN30-wide ingestion lands; SQLite proves the adapter\ncontract on a single ticker without Docker complexity.\n\nStorage layout: one row per (ticker, period_end, source_provider) so multi-\nsource reconciliation audit trail is preserved on disk. Read methods dedupe\nby (ticker, period_end), preferring SourceProvider.VNSTOCK over TCBS over\nothers (alphabetical tie-break) — keeps the BarRepository Protocol contract\nreturning one Bar per period.\n\n`save_many` is the ingestion-side write method; per S27 `bar_repository.py`\ndocstring, the Protocol intentionally has no write methods — adapters write\ndirectly through the concrete impl. Idempotent via ON CONFLICT REPLACE so\nrepeated CLI runs produce the same on-disk state.\n\nDecimal precision preserved by storing amounts as TEXT and reconstructing via\n`Decimal(str)` on read. SQLite numeric types would silently round; the\nfinancial-data-protocol does not allow that.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom collections.abc import Iterable\nfrom contextlib import closing\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom datetime import date, datetime\nfrom decimal import Decimal\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom packages.contracts import (\n    AdjustmentType,\n    BarId,\n    Currency,\n    Money,\n    SourceProvider,\n    Ticker,\n)\nfrom packages.domain.market_data.models import Bar\n\n__all__ = [\"SqliteBarRepository\"]\n\n\n_SCHEMA = \"\"\"\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS bars (\n    bar_id          TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n    ticker          TEXT NOT NULL,\n    period_end      TEXT NOT NULL,\n    filing_date     TEXT NOT NULL,\n    ingested_at     TEXT NOT NULL,\n    open_amount     TEXT NOT NULL,\n    high_amount     TEXT NOT NULL,\n    low_amount      TEXT NOT NULL,\n    close_amount    TEXT NOT NULL,\n    currency        TEXT NOT NULL,\n    volume          INTEGER NOT NULL,\n    foreign_buy     INTEGER NOT NULL,\n    foreign_sell    INTEGER NOT NULL,\n    adjustment_type TEXT NOT NULL,\n    source_provider TEXT NOT NULL,\n    UNIQUE(ticker, period_end, source_provider)\n);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_bars_ticker_period ON bars(ticker, period_end);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_bars_filing ON bars(filing_date);\n\"\"\"\n\n\n_SOURCE_PRIORITY = (\n    SourceProvider.VNSTOCK,\n    SourceProvider.TCBS,\n    SourceProvider.FIINPRO,\n    SourceProvider.SSI,\n    SourceProvider.WICHART,\n    SourceProvider.MANUAL,\n    SourceProvider.SCRAPED_OTHER,\n)\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass SqliteBarRepository:\n    \"\"\"SQLite-backed Bar persistence. Implements `BarRepository` Protocol.\"\"\"\n\n    db_path: Path\n\n    def __post_init__(self) -> None:\n        self.db_path = Path(self.db_path)\n        self.db_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        with closing(sqlite3.connect(str(self.db_path))) as conn:\n            conn.executescript(_SCHEMA)\n            conn.commit()\n\n    def save_many(self, bars: Iterable[Bar]) -> int:\n        rows = [self._to_row(b) for b in bars]\n        if not rows:\n            return 0\n        with closing(sqlite3.connect(str(self.db_path))) as conn:\n            conn.executemany(\n                \"\"\"\n                INSERT OR REPLACE INTO bars (\n                    bar_id, ticker, period_end, filing_date, ingested_at,\n                    open_amount, high_amount, low_amount, close_amount, currency,\n                    volume, foreign_buy, foreign_sell,\n                    adjustment_type, source_provider\n                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n                \"\"\",\n                rows,\n            )\n            conn.commit()\n        return len(rows)\n\n    def get_as_of(self, ticker: Ticker, as_of_date: date) -> list[Bar]:\n        sql = \"\"\"\n            SELECT * FROM bars\n            WHERE ticker = ?\n              AND period_end <= ?\n              AND filing_date <= ?\n            ORDER BY period_end ASC\n        \"\"\"\n        return self._dedupe(self._query(sql, (ticker.symbol, as_of_date.isoformat(), as_of_date.isoformat())))\n\n    def get_latest(self, ticker: Ticker) -> Bar | None:\n        bars = self._dedupe(\n            self._query(\n                \"SELECT * FROM bars WHERE ticker = ? ORDER BY period_end DESC LIMIT 10\",\n                (ticker.symbol,),\n            )\n        )\n        return bars[-1] if bars else None\n\n    def get_range_adjusted(\n        self, ticker: Ticker, start: date, end: date\n    ) -> list[Bar]:\n        sql = \"\"\"\n            SELECT * FROM bars\n            WHERE ticker = ?\n              AND period_end BETWEEN ? AND ?\n              AND adjustment_type = ?\n            ORDER BY period_end ASC\n        \"\"\"\n        return self._dedupe(\n            self._query(sql, (ticker.symbol, start.isoformat(), end.isoformat(), AdjustmentType.BOTH.value))\n        )\n\n    def count(self) -> int:\n        with closing(sqlite3.connect(str(self.db_path))) as conn:\n            cur = conn.execute(\"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM bars\")\n            return int(cur.fetchone()[0])\n\n    def _query(self, sql: str, params: tuple[object, ...]) -> list[Bar]:\n        with closing(sqlite3.connect(str(self.db_path))) as conn:\n            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n            cur = conn.execute(sql, params)\n            return [self._from_row(dict(r)) for r in cur.fetchall()]\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _dedupe(bars: list[Bar]) -> list[Bar]:\n        by_period: dict[date, Bar] = {}\n        priority = {sp: i for i, sp in enumerate(_SOURCE_PRIORITY)}\n        for b in bars:\n            existing = by_period.get(b.period_end)\n            if existing is None or priority.get(b.source_provider, 999) < priority.get(existing.source_provider, 999):\n                by_period[b.period_end] = b\n        return sorted(by_period.values(), key=lambda x: x.period_end)\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _to_row(b: Bar) -> tuple[object, ...]:\n        return (\n            b.bar_id,\n            b.ticker.symbol,\n            b.period_end.isoformat(),\n            b.filing_date.isoformat(),\n            b.ingested_at.isoformat(),\n            str(b.open.amount),\n            str(b.high.amount),\n            str(b.low.amount),\n            str(b.close.amount),\n            b.currency,\n            b.volume,\n            b.foreign_buy,\n            b.foreign_sell,\n            b.adjustment_type.value,\n            b.source_provider.value,\n        )\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _from_row(row: dict[str, object]) -> Bar:\n        currency = Currency(str(row[\"currency\"]))\n        return Bar(\n            ticker=Ticker(str(row[\"ticker\"])),\n            period_end=date.fromisoformat(str(row[\"period_end\"])),\n            filing_date=date.fromisoformat(str(row[\"filing_date\"])),\n            ingested_at=datetime.fromisoformat(str(row[\"ingested_at\"])),\n            open=Money(Decimal(str(row[\"open_amount\"])), currency),\n            high=Money(Decimal(str(row[\"high_amount\"])), currency),\n            low=Money(Decimal(str(row[\"low_amount\"])), currency),\n            close=Money(Decimal(str(row[\"close_amount\"])), currency),\n            volume=int(str(row[\"volume\"])),\n            foreign_buy=int(str(row[\"foreign_buy\"])),\n            foreign_sell=int(str(row[\"foreign_sell\"])),\n            adjustment_type=AdjustmentType(str(row[\"adjustment_type\"])),\n            source_provider=SourceProvider(str(row[\"source_provider\"])),\n            bar_id=BarId(str(row[\"bar_id\"])),\n        )\n","numLines":206,"startLine":1,"totalLines":206}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"56ec1bfd-7ce2-411e-a810-7237783bdffd","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"f314b500-bade-49f3-91a7-f08ec7c91411","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"6edbd8ff-3983-47e1-8f53-a91a73708f7d","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"3ce0f5ce-cede-4075-b864-0f5a79e07f65","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_012bmTR1MrUHa3RcpnXVPw6D","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"SsiAdapter — fetch DAILY Bars via SSI iBoard public chart API (httpx direct).\n2\t\n3\tImplements `BarProviderPort` (S32 Track A R2 closure). Per D-012 (S32):\n4\tTCBS public `stock-insight` endpoint family returned 404 across all probed\n5\tURL variants throughout S32; vnstock 4.0.2 `Quote` source backends are\n6\teffectively VCI-only in practice (TCBS/SSI/DNSE/FMARKET fail on\n7\tconstruction; MSN unreachable). SSI's iBoard chart-history endpoint at\n8\t`https://iboard-api.ssi.com.vn/statistics/charts/history` is publicly\n9\treachable and returned full 1-year OHLCV for 30/30 VN30 tickers (248 bars\n10\teach) during S32 coverage probe — restoring Rule 4 multi-source\n11\treconciliation discipline previously broken by R2.\n12\t\n13\tEndpoint shape (TradingView-style; `from`/`to` are unix epoch seconds):\n14\t\n15\t    GET https://iboard-api.ssi.com.vn/statistics/charts/history\n16\t        ?symbol=VHM&resolution=D&from=1714435200&to=1745971200\n17\t\n18\t    {\n19\t      \"code\": \"SUCCESS\",\n20\t      \"message\": \"...\",\n21\t      \"data\": {\n22\t        \"t\": [1714608000, 1714694400, ...],       # epoch seconds (UTC midnight)\n23\t        \"o\": [40.8, 40.5, ...],                    # open  (THOUSAND VND)\n24\t        \"h\": [41.25, 40.7, ...],                   # high  (THOUSAND VND)\n25\t        \"l\": [40.35, 40.1, ...],                   # low   (THOUSAND VND)\n26\t        \"c\": [41.15, 40.4, ...],                   # close (THOUSAND VND)\n27\t        \"v\": [4570000, 5102100, ...],              # volume (shares)\n28\t        \"s\": \"ok\",\n29\t        \"nextTime\": null\n30\t      }\n31\t    }\n32\t\n33\tPrices are in **thousand VND** (close=41.15 means 41,150 VND/share),\n34\tmatching the convention shared by VnstockAdapter so reconciliation does\n35\tnot need a unit-conversion step.\n36\t\n37\tPer Q-S28-3 + agent-notes \"stale data must propagate\": non-200 OR\n38\tunexpected payload → `SsiApiError`. The CLI orchestrator catches it and\nlogs single-source mode; never falls back silently. `http_get_fn` is\ninjected for test isolation per the same pattern as TcbsAdapter.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport time\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom datetime import UTC, date, datetime\nfrom decimal import Decimal\nfrom typing import Protocol, cast\n\nfrom packages.contracts import (\n    AdjustmentType,\n    Currency,\n    Money,\n    SourceProvider,\n    Ticker,\n)\nfrom packages.domain.market_data.models import Bar\n\n__all__ = [\"SsiAdapter\", \"SsiApiError\"]\n\n\n_SSI_BASE_URL = \"https://iboard-api.ssi.com.vn/statistics/charts/history\"\n_DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 10.0\n_VND_THOUSANDS_MULTIPLIER = Decimal(\"1000\")\n\nParamValue = str | int\n\n\nclass HttpResponse(Protocol):\n    \"\"\"Minimal httpx.Response surface the adapter consumes.\"\"\"\n\n    status_code: int\n\n    def json(self) -> object: ...\n\n\nclass HttpGetFn(Protocol):\n    \"\"\"Injectable HTTP GET. Production wraps httpx.get; tests pass fake.\"\"\"\n\n    def __call__(self, url: str, params: dict[str, ParamValue]) -> HttpResponse: ...\n\n\nclass SsiApiError(RuntimeError):\n    \"\"\"Raised when SSI iBoard API returns non-200 or unexpected payload shape.\"\"\"\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass SsiAdapter:\n    \"\"\"Fetch DAILY OHLCV via SSI iBoard public chart API.\n\n    `http_get_fn` is an injectable hook returning an object with `.status_code`\n    and `.json()` (httpx.Response shape). Production wiring constructs it from\n    httpx; tests pass a lambda returning a fake response so no network is\n    required per architecture.md § Testing fixture discipline.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    http_get_fn: HttpGetFn | None = None\n    timeout_seconds: float = _DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS\n    rate_limit_seconds: float = 1.0\n    _last_call_at: float = 0.0\n\n    def __post_init__(self) -> None:\n        if self.http_get_fn is None:\n            try:\n                import httpx\n            except ImportError as exc:\n                raise SsiApiError(\n                    \"httpx not installed; pip install httpx\"\n                ) from exc\n\n            def _http_get(url: str, params: dict[str, ParamValue]) -> HttpResponse:\n                return cast(\n                    HttpResponse,\n                    httpx.get(\n                        url,\n                        params=params,\n                        timeout=self.timeout_seconds,\n                        headers={\"User-Agent\": \"Mozilla/5.0 stockforge\"},\n                    ),\n                )\n\n            object.__setattr__(self, \"http_get_fn\", _http_get)\n\n    def fetch_daily(self, ticker: Ticker, start: date, end: date) -> list[Bar]:\n        if start > end:\n            raise ValueError(f\"start {start} > end {end}\")\n        self._respect_rate_limit()\n        assert self.http_get_fn is not None  # __post_init__ wires default\n\n        from_epoch = int(datetime.combine(start, datetime.min.time()).timestamp())\n        to_epoch = int(datetime.combine(end, datetime.min.time()).timestamp())\n        params: dict[str, ParamValue] = {\n            \"symbol\": ticker.symbol,\n            \"resolution\": \"D\",\n            \"from\": from_epoch,\n            \"to\": to_epoch,\n        }\n\n        response = self.http_get_fn(_SSI_BASE_URL, params)\n        status = getattr(response, \"status_code\", None)\n        if status != 200:\n            raise SsiApiError(\n                f\"SSI iBoard API returned status {status} for symbol={ticker.symbol}\"\n            )\n        payload = response.json()\n        if not isinstance(payload, dict) or \"data\" not in payload:\n            raise SsiApiError(f\"unexpected payload shape: {type(payload).__name__}\")\n        data = payload[\"data\"]\n        if not isinstance(data, dict):\n            raise SsiApiError(f\"unexpected data shape: {type(data).__name__}\")\n\n        times = data.get(\"t\") or []\n        opens = data.get(\"o\") or []\n        highs = data.get(\"h\") or []\n        lows = data.get(\"l\") or []\n        closes = data.get(\"c\") or []\n        volumes = data.get(\"v\") or []\n        if not isinstance(times, list):\n            raise SsiApiError(\"data.t is not a list\")\n        n = len(times)\n        if not all(isinstance(arr, list) and len(arr) == n for arr in (opens, highs, lows, closes, volumes)):\n            raise SsiApiError(\"OHLCV arrays length mismatch\")\n\n        ingested_at = datetime.now(UTC)\n        bars: list[Bar] = []\n        for i in range(n):\n            period_end = self._epoch_to_date(times[i])\n            if period_end is None or period_end < start or period_end > end:\n                continue\n            try:\n                bars.append(\n                    Bar(\n                        ticker=ticker,\n                        period_end=period_end,\n                        filing_date=period_end,\n                        ingested_at=ingested_at,\n                        open=self._money_thousand(opens[i]),\n                        high=self._money_thousand(highs[i]),\n                        low=self._money_thousand(lows[i]),\n                        close=self._money_thousand(closes[i]),\n                        volume=int(volumes[i]),\n                        foreign_buy=0,\n                        foreign_sell=0,\n                        adjustment_type=AdjustmentType.BOTH,\n                        source_provider=SourceProvider.SSI,\n                    )\n                )\n            except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:\n                raise SsiApiError(f\"row {i} malformed: {exc}\") from exc\n        bars.sort(key=lambda b: b.period_end)\n        return bars\n\n    def _respect_rate_limit(self) -> None:\n        elapsed = time.monotonic() - self._last_call_at\n        if 0 < elapsed < self.rate_limit_seconds:\n            time.sleep(self.rate_limit_seconds - elapsed)\n        object.__setattr__(self, \"_last_call_at\", time.monotonic())\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _epoch_to_date(value: object) -> date | None:\n        if value is None:\n            return None\n        if isinstance(value, int | float):\n            return datetime.fromtimestamp(float(value), tz=UTC).date()\n        return None\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _money_thousand(thousand_vnd: object) -> Money:\n        amount = Decimal(str(thousand_vnd)) * _VND_THOUSANDS_MULTIPLIER\n        return Money(amount, Production = vnstock Quote.history.\"\"\"\n50\t\n51\t    def __call__(self, *, symbol: str, start: str, end: str, interval: str) -> object: ...\n52\t\n53\t\n54\tclass _DataFrameLike(Protocol):\n55\t    \"\"\"Subset of pandas.DataFrame that VnstockAdapter consumes.\"\"\"\n56\t\n57\t    columns: list[str]\n58\t\n59\t    def iterrows(self) -> Iterator[tuple[int, dict[str, object]]]: ...\n60\t\n61\t\n62\tclass VnstockAdapterError(RuntimeError):\n63\t    \"\"\"Raised when vnstock returns an unexpected payload or transport fails.\"\"\"\n64\t\n65\t\n66\t@dataclass\n67\tclass VnstockAdapter:\n68\t    \"\"\"Fetch DAILY OHLCV via vnstock VCI source. Implements BarProviderPort.\n69\t\n70\t    `quote_history_fn` is injected for test isolation — production wiring uses\n71\t    the real `vnstock.api.quote.Quote(...).history`. Tests pass a lambda that\n72\t    returns a recorded DataFrame, enabling fixture-based testing with no\n73\t    network per architecture.md § Testing.\n74\t    \"\"\"\n75\t\n76\t    quote_history_fn: QuoteHistoryFn | None = None\n77\t    rate_limit_seconds: float = 1.0\n78\t    _last_call_at: float = 0.0\n79\t\n80\t    def __post_init__(self) -> None:\n81\t        if self.quote_history_fn is — fetch DAILY Bars via vnstock library (VCI source).\n\nImplements `BarProviderPort` (S28). Maps vnstock DataFrame rows to Bar entities\nwith full provenance per Rule 4 (source attribution): every emitted Bar has\n`source_provider=SourceProvider.VNSTOCK`, `adjustment_type=AdjustmentType.BOTH`\n(VCI returns adjusted prices by default; raw history is not exposed by the\nlibrary), `filing_date` set to `period_end` (EOD bars file same day they\nrepresent), `ingested_at` set to wall-clock at fetch time.\n\nvnstock 4.0.2 quote.history returns prices in **thousand VND** for VN equities\n(e.g., 124.0 means 124,000 VND/share). This adapter multiplies by 1000 so Bar\ncarries plain VND, matching domain expectations and SQLite storage.\n\nRate limiting: vnstock has no documented limit per library README; agent-notes\nprudence + Q-S28-2 → default `rate_limit_seconds=1.0` between calls when the\nadapter is invoked in a loop. Single-ticker single-call patterns (the S28 thin\nslice) do not loop, so the limit is informational.\n\nPer IMPL-S28-1 (S28 entry decision): vnstock 4.0.2 deprecated TCBS as a source\nof `Quote`. Two-source reconciliation pattern preserved by pairing this VCI\nadapter with the direct-HTTP TcbsAdapter that calls TCBS's public REST API.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport time\nfrom collections.abc import Iterator\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom datetime import UTC, date, datetime\nfrom decimal import Decimal\nfrom typing import Protocol, cast\n\nfrom packages.contracts import (\n    AdjustmentType,\n    Currency,\n    Money,\n    SourceProvider,\n    Ticker,\n)\nfrom packages.domain.market_data.models import Bar\n\n__all__ = [\"VnstockAdapter\", \"VnstockAdapterError\"]\n\n\n_VND_THOUSANDS_MULTIPLIER = Decimal(\"1000\")\n\n\nclass QuoteHistoryFn(Protocol):\n    \"\"\"Injectable history-fetch callable. Production = vnstock Quote.history.\"\"\"\n\n    def __call__(self, *, symbol: str, start: str, end: str, interval: str) -> object: ...\n\n\nclass _DataFrameLike(Protocol):\n    \"\"\"Subset of pandas.DataFrame that VnstockAdapter consumes.\"\"\"\n\n    columns: list[str]\n\n    def iterrows(self) -> Iterator[tuple[int, dict[str, object]]]: ...\n\n\nclass VnstockAdapterError(RuntimeError):\n    \"\"\"Raised when vnstock returns an unexpected payload or transport fails.\"\"\"\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass VnstockAdapter:\n    \"\"\"Fetch DAILY OHLCV via vnstock VCI source. Implements BarProviderPort.\n\n    `quote_history_fn` is injected for test isolation — production wiring uses\n    the real `vnstock.api.quote.Quote(...).history`. Tests pass a lambda that\n    returns a recorded DataFrame, enabling fixture-based testing with no\n    network per architecture.md § Testing.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    quote_history_fn: QuoteHistoryFn | None = None\n    rate_limit_seconds: float = 1.0\n    _last_call_at: float = 0.0\n\n    def __post_init__(self) -> None:\n        if self.quote_history_fn is None:\n            try:\n                from vnstock.api.quote import Quote  # type: ignore[import-untyped]\n            except ImportError as exc:\n                raise VnstockAdapterError(\n                    \"vnstock library not installed; pip install vnstock>=4.0.0\"\n                ) from exc\n\n            def _fetch(*, symbol: str, start: str, end: str, interval: str) -> object:\n                quote = Quote(symbol=symbol, source=\"VCI\")\n                return cast(object, quote.history(start=start, end=end, interval=interval))\n\n            object.__setattr__(self, \"quote_history_fn\", _fetch)\n\n    def fetch_daily(self, ticker: Ticker, start: date, end: date) -> list[Bar]:\n        if start > end:\n            raise ValueError(f\"start {start} > end {end}\")\n        self._respect_rate_limit()\n        assert self.quote_history_fn is not None  # __post_init__ wires default\n\n        df = self.quote_history_fn(\n            symbol=ticker.symbol,\n            start=start.isoformat(),\n            end=end.isoformat(),\n            interval=\"1D\",\n        )\n\n        ingested_at = datetime.now(UTC)\n        bars: list[Bar] = []\n        for row in self._iter_rows(df):\n            period_end = self._coerce_date(row[\"time\"])\n            if period_end > date.today() or period_end > end or period_end < start:\n                continue\n            bars.append(\n                Bar(\n                    ticker=ticker,\n                    period_end=period_end,\n                    filing_date=period_end,\n                    ingested_at=ingested_at,\n                    open=self._money(row[\"open\"]),\n                    high=self._money(row[\"high\"]),\n                    low=self._money(row[\"low\"]),\n                    close=self._money(row[\"close\"]),\n                    volume=int(str(row[\"volume\"])),\n                    foreign_buy=0,\n                    foreign_sell=0,\n                    adjustment_type=AdjustmentType.BOTH,\n                    source_provider=SourceProvider.VNSTOCK,\n                )\n            )\n        bars.sort(key=lambda b: b.period_end)\n        return bars\n\n    def _respect_rate_limit(self) -> None:\n        elapsed = time.monotonic() - self._last_call_at\n        if 0 < elapsed < self.rate_limit_seconds:\n            time.sleep(self.rate_limit_seconds - elapsed)\n        object.__setattr__(self, \"_last_call_at\", time.monotonic())\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _iter_rows(df: object) -> Iterator[dict[str, object]]:\n        if hasattr(df, \"iterrows\"):\n            df_typed = cast(_DataFrameLike, df)\n            for _idx, row in df_typed.iterrows():\n                yield {col: row[col] for col in df_typed.columns}\n            return\n        if isinstance(df, list):\n            for row in df:\n                yield cast(dict[str, object], row)\n            return\n        raise VnstockAdapterError(f\"unexpected vnstock payload type {type(df).__name__}\")\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _coerce_date(value: object) -> date:\n        if isinstance(value, date) and not isinstance(value, datetime):\n            return value\n        if isinstance(value, datetime):\n            return value.date()\n        if isinstance(value, str):\n            return datetime.strptime(value[:10], \"%Y-%m-%d\").date()\n        if hasattr(value, \"to_pydatetime\"):\n            converted = value.to_pydatetime()\n            return cast(datetime, converted).date()\n        raise VnstockAdapterError(f\"cannot coerce {value!r} to date\")\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _money(thousand_vnd: object) -> Money:\n        amount = Decimal(str(thousand_vnd)) * _VND_THOUSANDS_MULTIPLIER\n        return Money(amount, Currency.VND)\n","numLines":170,"startLine":1,"totalLines":170}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"31662cc7-777a-4c15-ab0d-95dd02f0b3d5","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"f314b500-bade-49f3-91a7-f08ec7c91411","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

Adheres to architecture.md §\nTesting fixture discipline + master-plan 004 § S28 success criteria #3.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom datetime import UTC, date, datetime, timedelta\nfrom decimal import Decimal\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom packages.contracts import (\n    AdjustmentType,\n    Currency,\n    CurrencyMismatchError,\n    Money,\n    SourceProvider,\n    Ticker,\n)\nfrom packages.domain.market_data.models import Bar\nfrom packages.infrastructure.market_data import (\n    ReconciliationConfidence,\n    ReconciliationService,\n    SqliteBarRepository,\n    SsiAdapter,\n    SsiApiError,\n    TcbsAdapter,\n    TcbsApiError,\n    VnstockAdapter,\n)\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# helpers\n\n\ndef _bar(\n    *,\n    ticker: str = \"VHM\",\n    period_end: date,\n    close_amount: str = \"120000\",\n    source: SourceProvider = SourceProvider.VNSTOCK,\n    adjustment: AdjustmentType = AdjustmentType.BOTH,\n    currency: Currency = Currency.VND,\n) -> Bar:\n    return Bar(\n        ticker=Ticker(ticker),\n        period_end=period_end,\n        filing_date=period_end,\n        ingested_at=datetime(2026, 4, 30, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC),\n        open=Money(Decimal(close_amount), currency),\n        high=Money(Decimal(close_amount) + Decimal(\"500\"), currency),\n        low=Money(Decimal(close_amount) - Decimal(\"500\"), currency),\n        close=Money(Decimal(close_amount), currency),\n        volume=1_000_000,\n        foreign_buy=0,\n        foreign_sell=0,\n        adjustment_type=adjustment,\n        source_provider=source,\n    )\n\n\nclass _FakeDataFrame:\n    \"\"\"Minimal DataFrame stand-in for VnstockAdapter tests.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, rows: list[dict[str, object]]) -> None:\n        self.rows = rows\n        self.columns = list(rows[0].keys()) if rows else []\n\n    def iterrows(self) -> object:\n        return iter([(i, r) for i, r in enumerate(self.rows)])\n\n\nclass _FakeResponse:\n    def __init__(self, status_code: int, payload: object) -> None:\n        self.status_code = status_code\n        self._payload = payload\n\n    def json(self) -> object:\n        return self._payload\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# VnstockAdapter tests\n\n\nclass TestVnstockAdapter:\n    def _adapter(self, df: object) -> VnstockAdapter:\n        return VnstockAdapter(quote_history_fn=lambda **_kw: df, rate_limit_seconds=0)\n\n    def test_fetch_maps_dataframe_to_bars(self) -> None:\n        df = _FakeDataFrame(\n            [\n                {\"time\": date(2026, 4, 28), \"open\": 124.0, \"high\": 124.5, \"low\": 122.0, \"close\": 123.5, \"volume\": 2_500_000},\n                {\"time\": date(2026, 4, 29), \"open\": 123.5, \"high\": 125.0, \"low\": 122.0, \"close\": 124.0, \"volume\": 3_100_000},\n            ]\n        )","numLines":100,"startLine":1,"totalLines":473}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"d67737e8-4169-4131-92a2-0c050c9edab5","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"f314b500-bade-49f3-91a7-f08ec7c91411","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

T+2.5, Room ngoại, Sàn HOSE/HNX/UPCoM, Trần, Sàn, Phiên ATO/ATC, Tỷ giá USD/VND)\n23:> **Why these Rules and not others**: glossary v1.0 surfaced 8 critical VN-domain terms (T+2.5, Room ngoại, Đội lái, Mua chủ động, Bán chủ động, Sàn HOSE/HNX/UPCoM, Phiên ATO/ATC, Tỷ giá USD/VND). Rules 12-15 cover the 4 that are data-integrity-binding for Phase 1+ (settlement timing, foreign-cap detection, sàn-tier data quality, FX point-in-time). Đội lái + Mua/Bán chủ động + ATO/ATC are Phase 2+ (intraday + Tier 4 work) — deferred.\n60:- Source provenance: HOSE listed-stocks portal + vnstock `room_ngoai()` endpoint + TCBS reconciliation per Rule 4. Rule 4 reconciliation tolerance = 1% of cap (NOT 1% of saturation_pct — different denominators).\n69:## Rule 14 — Sàn HOSE / HNX / UPCoM Data-Quality Tiering\n73:VN listed equities split across 3 exchanges (Sàn): HOSE (HCMC; tier-1 large caps; strictest disclosure), HNX (Hanoi; tier-2; mid-caps), UPCoM (over-the-counter board; tier-3; micro-caps + pre-listing transitional). Data quality, filing cadence, and cross-source reconciliation tolerance differ by Sàn. Treating UPCoM Bars with the same trust as HOSE Bars produces false-positive backtest signals on noisy UPCoM data.\n77:- Every `Ticker` value object infers Sàn at construction (regex on listing-status feed: 3-letter alpha → HOSE; 3-letter alpha + HNX flag → HNX; UPCoM has explicit ticker namespace).\n78:- Every `Bar` carries `sàn` field (NEW; values: HOSE | HNX | UPCoM). Field is denormalized from Ticker for query performance (Rule 4 reconciliation runs per-Sàn).\n79:- Reconciliation tolerance per Rule 4 is Sàn-tiered:\n80:  - HOSE: ±1% (existing tolerance preserved)\n81:  - HNX: ±2% (mid-cap, slightly noisier filings)\n82:  - UPCoM: ±5% (micro-cap, frequent stale data; Sàn-tier downgrade flag mandatory)\n83:- Phase 1 thin-slice scope locks to HOSE only (VHM is HOSE; spec 000 § A.5 Out-of-Scope item 4).\n84:- Charter \"First Sub-Scope\" mid-cap band (vốn hóa 2,000-50,000 tỷ VND) implicitly excludes most UPCoM stocks; this Rule codifies the data-quality reason.\n88:`Bar.__post_init__` validates `sàn ∈ {HOSE, HNX, UPCoM}`. `ReconciliationService.tolerance_for(bar)` returns Sàn-tier tolerance. Backtest universe construction (per Rule 2) MUST tag `Sàn` per row; CI gate (Phase 2+) warns if backtest dataset mixes Sàn without explicit tier-aware logic."}]},"uuid":"34ccc80d-084e-4e5f-ad18-0c6b3f50c7a6","timestamp":"2026-04-30T14:59:51.493Z","toolUseResult":{"mode":"content","numFiles":0,"filenames":[],"content":"6:  - PROJECT_CHARTER.md § First Sub-Scope (HOSE/HNX/UPCoM, vốn hóa 2,000-50,000 tỷ VND, position sizing 5-15%, holding 1m-24m)\n7:  - agent-workspace/ubiquitous-language/glossary.md v1.0 (S25 deliverable; 42 VN terms incl.
