TestTcbsAdapter:\n    def _adapter(self, response: _FakeResponse) -> TcbsAdapter:\n        return TcbsAdapter(http_get_fn=lambda _url, _params: response, rate_limit_seconds=0)\n\n    def test_fetch_maps_payload_to_bars(self) -> None:\n        payload = {\n            \"data\": [\n                {\"open\": 124000, \"high\": 124500, \"low\": 122000, \"close\": 123500, \"volume\": 2_500_000, \"tradingDate\": \"2026-04-28T00:00:00.000Z\"},\n                {\"open\": 123500, \"high\": 125000, \"low\": 122000, \"close\": 124000, \"volume\": 3_100_000, \"tradingDate\": \"2026-04-29T00:00:00.000Z\"},\n            ],\n            \"ticker\": \"VHM\",\n        }\n        bars = self._adapter(_FakeResponse(200, payload)).fetch_daily(Ticker(\"VHM\"), date(2026, 4, 1), date(2026, 4, 30))\n        assert len(bars) == 2\n        assert bars[0].close.amount == Decimal(\"123500\")\n        assert bars[0].source_provider == SourceProvider.TCBS\n\n    def test_non_200_raises_TcbsApiError(self) -> None:\n        with pytest.raises(TcbsApiError, match=\"status\"):\n            self._adapter(_FakeResponse(500, {})).fetch_daily(Ticker(\"VHM\"), date(2026, 4, 1), date(2026, 4, 30))\n\n    def test_unexpected_payload_raises(self) -> None:\n        with pytest.raises(TcbsApiError, match=\"payload shape\"):\n            self._adapter(_FakeResponse(200, [\"not\", \"a\", \"dict\"])).fetch_daily(Ticker(\"VHM\"), date(2026, 4, 1), date(2026, 4, 30))\n\n    def test_filters_outside_range(self) -> None:\n        payload = {\n            \"data\": [\n                {\"open\": 1, \"high\": 1, \"low\": 1, \"close\": 1, \"volume\": 1, \"tradingDate\": \"2026-01-15T00:00:00.000Z\"},\n                {\"open\": 2, \"high\": 2, \"low\": 2, \"close\": 2, \"volume\": 2, \"tradingDate\": \"2026-04-15T00:00:00.000Z\"},\n            ]\n        }\n        bars = self._adapter(_FakeResponse(200, payload)).fetch_daily(Ticker(\"VHM\"), date(2026, 4, 1), date(2026, 4, 30))\n        assert len(bars) == 1\n        assert bars[0].period_end == date(2026, 4, 15)\n\n    def test_invalid_start_after_end(self) -> None:\n        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=\"start\"):\n            self._adapter(_FakeResponse(200, {\"data\": []})).fetch_daily(\n                Ticker(\"VHM\"), date(2026, 4, 30), date(2026, 4, 1)\n            )\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# SsiAdapter tests (S32 Track A R2 closure; D-012 A3 strategy)\n\n\nclass TestSsiAdapter:\n    def _adapter(self, response: _FakeResponse) -> SsiAdapter:\n        return SsiAdapter(http_get_fn=lambda _url, _params: response, rate_limit_seconds=0)\n\n    def _payload(self, t_epochs: list[int], closes: list[float]) -> dict[str, object]:\n        n = len(t_epochs)\n        return {\n            \"code\": \"SUCCESS\",\n            \"message\": \"ok\",\n            \"data\": {\n                \"t\": list(t_epochs),\n                \"o\": [c - 0.5 for c in closes],\n                \"h\": [c + 0.3 for c in closes],\n                \"l\": [c - 0.7 for c in closes],\n                \"c\": list(closes),\n                \"v\": [1_000_000 + i for i in range(n)],\n                \"s\": \"ok\",\n                \"nextTime\": None,\n            },\n        }\n\n    def test_fetch_maps_payload_to_bars_thousand_vnd(self) -> None:\n        # 2026-04-28 UTC midnight, 2026-04-29 UTC midnight\n        t1 = int(datetime(2026, 4, 28, tzinfo=UTC).timestamp())\n        t2 = int(datetime(2026, 4, 29, tzinfo=UTC).timestamp())\n        payload = self._payload([t1, t2], [123.5, 124.0])\n        bars = self._adapter(_FakeResponse(200, payload)).fetch_daily(\n            Ticker(\"VHM\"), date(2026, 4, 1), date(2026, 4, 30)\n        )\n        assert len(bars) == 2\n        # thousand-VND multiplier (123.5 → 123500)\n        assert bars[0].close.amount == Decimal(\"123500.0\")\n        assert bars[0].source_provider == SourceProvider.SSI\n        assert bars[0].adjustment_type == AdjustmentType.BOTH\n        assert bars[0].volume == 1_000_000\n\n    def test_non_200_raises_SsiApiError(self) -> None:\n        with pytest.raises(SsiApiError, match=\"status\"):\n            self._adapter(_FakeResponse(503, {})).fetch_daily(\n                Ticker(\"VHM\"), date(2026, 4, 1), date(2026, 4, 30)\n            )\n\n    def test_unexpected_payload_shape_raises(self) -> None:\n        with pytest.raises(SsiApiError, match=\"payload shape\"):\n            self._adapter(_FakeResponse(200, [\"not\", \"a\", \"dict\"])).fetch_daily(\n                Ticker(\"VHM\"), date(2026, 4, 1), date(2026, 4, 30)\n            )\n\n    def test_missing_data_key_raises(self) -> None:\n        with pytest.raises(SsiApiError, match=\"payload shape\"):\n            self._adapter(_FakeResponse(200, {\"code\": \"SUCCESS\"})).fetch_daily(\n                Ticker(\"VHM\"), date(2026, 4, 1), date(2026, 4, 30)\n            )\n\n    def test_data_not_dict_raises(self) -> None:\n        with pytest.raises(SsiApiError, match=\"data shape\"):\n            self._adapter(_FakeResponse(200, {\"data\": \"string-not-dict\"})).fetch_daily(\n                Ticker(\"VHM\"), date(2026, 4, 1), date(2026, 4, 30)\n            )\n\n    def test_array_length_mismatch_raises(self) -> None:\n        bad = {\n            \"data\": {\n                \"t\": [1, 2, 3],\n                \"o\": [1.0, 2.0],\n                \"h\": [1.0, 2.0, 3.0],\n                \"l\": [1.0, 2.0, 3.0],\n                \"c\": [1.0, 2.0, 3.0],\n                \"v\": [1, 2, 3],\n            }\n        }\n        with pytest.raises(SsiApiError, match=\"length mismatch\"):\n            self._adapter(_FakeResponse(200, bad)).fetch_daily(\n                Ticker(\"VHM\"), date(2026, 4, 1), date(2026, 4, 30)\n            )\n\n    def test_filters_outside_range(self) -> None:\n        early = int(datetime(2026, 1, 15, tzinfo=UTC).timestamp())\n        inside = int(datetime(2026, 4, 15, tzinfo=UTC).timestamp())\n        payload =     assert len(bars) == 1\n        assert bars[0].period_end == date(2026, 4, 15)\n\n    def test_invalid_start_after_end(self) -> None:\n        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=\"start\"):\n payload)).fetch_daily(\n            Ticker(\"VHM\"), date(2026, 4, 1), date(2026, 4, 30)\n        )\n        assert all(b.source_provider == SourceProvider.SSI for b in bars)\n\n    def test_volume_coerced_to_int(self) -> None:\n        t = int(datetime(2026, 4, 28, tzinfo=UTC).timestamp())\n        payload = {\"data\": {\"t\": [t], \"o\": [1.0], \"h\": [1.0], \"l\": [1.0], \"c\": [1.0], \"v\": [4570000.0]}}\n        bars = self._adapter(_FakeResponse(200, payload)).fetch_daily(\n            Ticker(\"VHM\"), date(2026, 4, 1), date(2026, 4, 30)\n        )\n        assert bars[0].volume == 4_570_000\n        assert isinstance(bars[0].volume, int)\n\n    def test_decimal_thousand_multiplier_precision(self) -> None:\n        t = int(datetime(2026, 4, 28, tzinfo=UTC).timestamp())\n        payload = self._payload([t], [41.15])\n        bars = self._adapter(_FakeResponse(200, payload)).fetch_daily(\n            Ticker(\"VHM\"), date(2026, 4, 1), date(2026, 4, 30)\n        )\n        assert bars[0].close.amount == Decimal(\"41150.00\")  # 41.15 * 1000\n        assert bars[0].close.currency == Currency.VND\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Cross-source reconciliation tests (S32 Track A — Rule 4 dual-source)\n\n\nclass TestCrossSourceReconciliation:\n    \"\"\"Verify VnstockAdapter (VCI) + SsiAdapter (iBoard) yield reconcilable Bars.\"\"\"\n\n    def test_vci_vs_ssi_within_tolerance_HIGH(self) -> None:\n        d = date(2026, 4, 28)\n        vci = [_bar(period_end=d, close_amount=\"100000\", source=SourceProvider.VNSTOCK)]\n        ssi = [_bar(period_end=d, close_amount=\"100500\", source=SourceProvider.SSI)]\n        rows = ReconciliationService().reconcile(vci, ssi)\n        assert len(rows) == 1\n        assert rows[0].confidence == ReconciliationConfidence.HIGH\n        assert rows[0].sources_present == (SourceProvider.VNSTOCK, SourceProvider.SSI)\n        assert rows[0].close_reconciled.amount == Decimal(\"100250\")\n\n    def test_vci_only_emits_SINGLE_SOURCE_VNSTOCK(self) -> None:\n        d = date(2026, 4, 28)\n        rows = ReconciliationService().reconcile(\n            [_bar(period_end=d, source=SourceProvider.VNSTOCK)], []\n        )\n        assert rows[0].confidence == ReconciliationConfidence.SINGLE_SOURCE\n        assert rows[0].sources_present == (SourceProvider.VNSTOCK,)\n\n    def test_ssi_only_emits_SINGLE_SOURCE_SSI(self) -> None:\n        d = date(2026, 4, 28)\n        rows = ReconciliationService().reconcile(\n            [], [_bar(period_end=d, source=SourceProvider.SSI)]\n        )\n        assert rows[0].confidence == ReconciliationConfidence.SINGLE_SOURCE\n        assert rows[0].sources_present == (SourceProvider.SSI,)\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# ReconciliationService tests\n\n\nclass TestReconciliationService:\n    def test_within_1pct_HIGH_confidence_mean(self) -> None:\n        d = date(2026, 4, 28)\n        primary = [_bar(period_end=d, close_amount=\"100000\")]\n        secondary = [_bar(period_end=d, close_amount=\"100500\", source=SourceProvider.TCBS)]\n        rows = ReconciliationService().reconcile(primary, secondary)\n        assert len(rows) == 1\n        assert rows[0].confidence == ReconciliationConfidence.HIGH\n        assert rows[0].close_reconciled.amount == Decimal(\"100250\")  # mean\n\n    def test_outside_1pct_LOW_confidence_uses_primary(self) -> None:\n        d = date(2026, 4, 28)\n        primary = [_bar(period_end=d, close_amount=\"100000\")]\n        secondary = [_bar(period_end=d, close_amount=\"105000\", source=SourceProvider.TCBS)]\n        rows = ReconciliationService().reconcile(primary, secondary)\n        assert rows[0].confidence == ReconciliationConfidence.LOW\n        assert rows[0].close_reconciled.amount == Decimal(\"100000\")  # primary kept\n\n    def test_only_primary_present_SINGLE_SOURCE(self) -> None:\n        rows = ReconciliationService().reconcile([_bar(period_end=date(2026, 4, 28))], [])\n        assert rows[0].confidence == ReconciliationConfidence.SINGLE_SOURCE\n        assert rows[0].sources_present == (SourceProvider.VNSTOCK,)\n\n    def test_only_secondary_present_SINGLE_SOURCE(self) -> None:\n        rows = ReconciliationService().reconcile(\n            [], [_bar(period_end=date(2026, 4, 28), source=SourceProvider.TCBS)]\n        )\n        assert rows[0].confidence == ReconciliationConfidence.SINGLE_SOURCE\n        assert rows[0].sources_present == (SourceProvider.TCBS,)\n\n    def test_currency_mismatch_raises(self) -> None:\n        d = date(2026, 4, 28)\n        primary = [_bar(period_end=d, close_amount=\"100000\", currency=Currency.VND)]\n        secondary = [_bar(period_end=d, close_amount=\"100\", source=SourceProvider.TCBS, currency=Currency.USD)]\n        with pytest.raises(CurrencyMismatchError):\n            ReconciliationService().reconcile(primary, secondary)\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# SqliteBarRepository tests\n\n\nclass TestSqliteBarRepository:\n    def _repo(self, tmp_path: Path) -> SqliteBarRepository:\n        return SqliteBarRepository(db_path=tmp_path / \"bars.sqlite\")\n\n    def test_save_then_get_latest(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n        repo = self._repo(tmp_path)\n        bars = [_bar(period_end=date(2026, 4, 28)), _bar(period_end=date(2026, 4, 29))]\n        repo.save_many(bars)\n        latest = repo.get_latest(Ticker(\"VHM\"))\n        assert latest is not None\n        assert latest.period_end == date(2026, 4, 29)\n\n    def test_get_as_of_filters_by_filing_date(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n        repo = self._repo(tmp_path)\n        repo.save_many(\n            [_bar(period_end=date(2026, 4, 15)), _bar(period_end=date(2026, 4, 25))]\n        )\n        as_of = repo.get_as_of(Ticker(\"VHM\"), date(2026, 4, 20))\n        assert len(as_of) == 1\n        assert as_of[0].period_end == date(2026, 4, 15)\n\n    def test_get_range_adjusted_BOTH_only(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n        repo = self._repo(tmp_path)\n        repo.save_many(\n            [\n                _bar(period_end=date(2026, 4, 15), adjustment=AdjustmentType.BOTH),\n                _bar(period_end=date(2026, 4, 16), adjustment=AdjustmentType.NONE),\n            ]\n        )\n        rows = repo.get_range_adjusted(Ticker(\"VHM\"), date(2026, 4, 1), date(2026, 4, 30))\n        assert len(rows) == 1\n        assert rows[0].adjustment_type == AdjustmentType.BOTH\n\n    def test_save_many_idempotent(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n        repo = self._repo(tmp_path)\n        b = _bar(period_end=date(2026, 4, 28))\n        repo.save_many([b])\n        repo.save_many([b])\n        assert repo.count() == 1\n\n    def test_dedupe_prefers_VNSTOCK_over_TCBS(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n        repo = self._repo(tmp_path)\n        d = date(2026, 4, 28)\n        repo.save_many(\n            [\n                _bar(period_end=d, close_amount=\"100000\", source=SourceProvider.VNSTOCK),\n                _bar(period_end=d, close_amount=\"100500\", source=SourceProvider.TCBS),\n            ]\n        )\n        latest = repo.get_latest(Ticker(\"VHM\"))\n        assert latest is not None\n        assert latest.source_provider == SourceProvider.VNSTOCK\n        assert repo.count() == 2  # both rows persisted; read dedupes\n\n    def test_decimal_precision_preserved(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n        repo = self._repo(tmp_path)\n        precise = _bar(period_end=date(2026, 4, 28), close_amount=\"123456.789\")\n        repo.save_many([precise])\n        latest = repo.get_latest(Ticker(\"VHM\"))\n        assert latest is not None\n        assert latest.close.amount == {"parentUuid":"33c474d1-608d-4a8b-b905-39373da7bc13","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"3ce0f5ce-cede-4075-b864-0f5a79e07f65","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_018STa8qeeGTzAA3W9ZYLQPj","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Ticker — VN equity symbol value object.\n2\t\n3\tPhase 1 scope: HOSE + HNX + UPCoM listed equities. All VN tickers are\n4\t3-character codes (A-Z or 0-9). Examples:\n5\t- HOSE: VHM, VIC, VPB, HPG, FPT, MWG (3 uppercase letters)\n6\t- HNX:  ACB, SHB, PVS (3 uppercase letters)\n7\t- UPCoM: BSR, MSR (3 uppercase letters); mixed alphanumeric also exists\n8\t\n9\tPer agent-workspace/ubiquitous-language/glossary.md v1.0 (S25 output) §\n10\t\"Ticker\" entry: 3-character code, A-Z + 0-9, case-normalized to uppercase.\n11\t\n12\tSource: PROJECT_CHARTER.md § First Sub-Scope (\"HOSE/HNX/UPCoM\"); validated\n13\tby glossary v1.0; binding from Phase 1 entry 2026-04-30.\n14\t\"\"\"\n15\t\n16\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n17\t\n18\timport re\n19\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n20\t\n21\t__all__ = [\"Ticker\", \"InvalidTickerError\"]\n22\t\n23\t\n24\t_TICKER_PATTERN = re.compile(r\"^[A-Z0-9]{3}$\")\n25\t\n26\t\n27\tclass InvalidTickerError(ValueError):\n28\t    \"\"\"Raised when a ticker symbol fails VN-format validation.\"\"\"\n29\t\n30\t    def __init__(self, raw: str, reason: str) -> None:\n31\t        super().__init__(f\"invalid ticker {raw!r}: {reason}\")\n32\t        self.raw = raw\n33\t        self.reason = reason\n34\t\n35\t\n36\t@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\n37\tclass Ticker:\n38\t    \"\"\"Immutable VN equity ticker symbol. Case-normalized to uppercase.\n39\t\n40\t    Validates 3-character A-Z/0-9 pattern in __post_init__. Construction with\n41\t    any other shape raises InvalidTickerError — invalid states are\n42\t    unrepresentable per architecture.md § Domain Model Rules.\n43\t    \"\"\"\n44\t\n45\t    symbol: str\n46\t\n47\t    def __post_init__(self) -> None:\n48\t        if not isinstance(self.symbol, str):\n49\t            raise InvalidTickerError(\n50\t                str(self.symbol), f\"expected str, got {type(self.symbol).__name__}\"\n51\t            )\n52\t        upper = self.symbol.upper()\n53\t        if not _TICKER_PATTERN.match(upper):\n54\t            raise InvalidTickerError(\n55\t                self.symbol,\n56\t                \"must be exactly 3 characters of A-Z or 0-9 (VN HOSE/HNX/UPCoM convention)\",\n57\t            )\n58\t        if upper != self.symbol:\n59\t            object.__setattr__(self, \"symbol\", upper)\n60\t\n61\t    def __str__(self) -> str:\n62\t        return self.symbol\n63\t"}]},"uuid":"e06a6034-d595-453b-8c1d-ac89c253d630","timestamp":"2026-04-30T15:00:03.397Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\packages\\contracts\\types\\ticker.py","content":"\"\"\"Ticker — VN equity symbol value object.\n\nPhase 1 scope: HOSE + HNX + UPCoM listed equities. All VN tickers are\n3-character codes (A-Z or 0-9). Examples:\n- HOSE: VHM, VIC, VPB, HPG, FPT, MWG (3 uppercase letters)\n- HNX:  ACB, SHB, PVS (3 uppercase letters)\n- UPCoM: BSR, MSR (3 uppercase letters); mixed alphanumeric also exists\n\nPer agent-workspace/ubiquitous-language/glossary.md v1.0 (S25 output) §\n\"Ticker\" entry: 3-character code, A-Z + 0-9, case-normalized to uppercase.\n\nSource: PROJECT_CHARTER.md § First Sub-Scope (\"HOSE/HNX/UPCoM\"); validated\nby glossary v1.0; binding from Phase 1 entry 2026-04-30.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n\n__all__ = [\"Ticker\", \"InvalidTickerError\"]\n\n\n_TICKER_PATTERN = re.compile(r\"^[A-Z0-9]{3}$\")\n\n\nclass InvalidTickerError(ValueError):\n    \"\"\"Raised when a ticker symbol fails VN-format validation.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, raw: str, reason: str) -> None:\n        super().__init__(f\"invalid ticker {raw!r}: {reason}\")\n        self.raw = raw\n        self.reason = reason\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\nclass Ticker:\n    \"\"\"Immutable VN equity ticker symbol. Case-normalized to uppercase.\n\n    Validates 3-character A-Z/0-9 pattern in __post_init__. Construction with\n    any other shape raises InvalidTickerError — invalid states are\n    unrepresentable per architecture.md § Domain Model Rules.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    symbol: str\n\n    def __post_init__(self) -> None:\n        if not isinstance(self.symbol, str):\n            raise InvalidTickerError(\n                str(self.symbol), f\"expected str, got {type(self.symbol).__name__}\"\n            )\n        upper = self.symbol.upper()\n        if not _TICKER_PATTERN.match(upper):\n            raise InvalidTickerError(\n                self.symbol,\n                \"must be exactly 3 characters of A-Z or 0-9 (VN HOSE/HNX/UPCoM convention)\",\n            )\n        if upper != self.symbol:\n            object.__setattr__(self, \"symbol\", upper)\n\n    def __str__(self) -> str:\n        return self.symbol\n","numLines":63,"startLine":1,"totalLines":63}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"33c474d1-608d-4a8b-b905-39373da7bc13","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"f314b500-bade-49f3-91a7-f08ec7c91411","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"f4288552-46f7-4f67-a112-63c8d8c12228","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"3ce0f5ce-cede-4075-b864-0f5a79e07f65","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01N7tcRt4Yt4bDV3skHDa3bL","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Bar — atomic OHLCV time-series row for a single ticker + timeframe.\n2\t\n3\tPhase 1 thin-slice scope: DAILY EOD bars only. Intraday (ATO/ATC, Mua chủ\n4\tđộng/Bán chủ động fields) deferred to Phase 2 per spec 000 § A.5.\n5\t\n6\tInvariants enforced in __post_init__:\n7\t- BR-1 (spec 000): period_end ≤ today (no future bars)\n8\t- BR-1: filing_date ≤ ingested_at (ingestion cannot precede filing)\n9\t- BR-1 + I-S4: adjustment_type non-None (Enum guarantees)\n10\t- I-S5 + Rule 4: source_provider non-None (Enum guarantees)\n11\t- BR-3 + I-S6: open/high/low/close all share the same currency\n12\t- OHLC ordering: low ≤ open ≤ high; low ≤ close ≤ high; low ≤ high\n13\t- volume + foreign_buy + foreign_sell ≥ 0\n14\t\n15\tSource: agent-workspace/constitution/architecture.md § BC-1 Market Data;\n16\tfinancial-data-protocol.md Rule 1 + Rule 3 + Rule 4 + Rule 5; invariants.md\n17\tI-S2 + I-S4 + I-S5 + I-S6; spec 000 § B.4.\n18\t\"\"\"\n19\t\n20\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n21\t\n22\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n23\tfrom datetime import UTC, date, datetime, timedelta\n24\tfrom decimal import Decimal\n25\t\n26\tfrom packages.contracts import (\n27\t    AdjustmentType,\n28\t    BarId,\n29\t    Money,\n30\t    PriceSnapshot,\n31\t    SourceProvider,\n32\t    Ticker,\n33\t    new_bar_id,\n34\t)\n35\t\n36\t__all__ = [\"Bar\", \"BarInvariantError\"]\n37\t\n38\t\n39\t_DEFAULT_STALE_DAYS = 7\n40\t\n41\t\n42\tclass BarInvariantError(ValueError):\n43\t    \"\"\"Raised when a Bar is constructed with values violating BR-1 / I-S2 / I-S4 / I-S6.\"\"\"\n44\t\n45\t\n46\t@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\n47\tclass Bar:\n48\t    \"\"\"Immutable OHLCV bar. Rich entity per architecture.md § Domain Model Rules.\n49\t\n50\t    Methods (`is_stale`, `apply_split`) preserve immutability — apply_split\n51\t    returns a NEW Bar rather than mutating; is_stale is pure read.\n52\t    \"\"\"\n53\t\n54\t    ticker: Ticker\n55\t    period_end: date\n56\t    filing_date: date\n57\t    ingested_at: datetime\n58\t    open: Money\n59\t    high: Money\n60\t    low: Money\n61\t    close: Money\n62\t    volume: int\n63\t    foreign_buy: int\n64\t    foreign_sell: int\n65\t    adjustment_type: AdjustmentType\n66\t    source_provider: SourceProvider\n67\t    bar_id: BarId = field(default_factory=new_bar_id)\n68\t\n69\t    def __post_init__(self) -> None:\n70\t        today = datetime.now(UTC).date()\n71\t        if self.period_end > today:\n72\t            raise BarInvariantError(\n73\t                f\"period_end {self.period_end} > today {today} (look-ahead bias; I-S2)\"\n74\t            )\n75\t        if self.filing_date > self.ingested_at.date():\n76\t            raise BarInvariantError(\n77\t                f\"filing_date {self.filing_date} > ingested_at {self.ingested_at.date()} \"\n78\t                f\"(ingestion cannot precede filing; Rule 1)\"\n79\t            )\n80\t        currencies = {self.open.currency, self.high.currency, self.low.currency, self.close.currency}\n81\t        if len(currencies) > 1:\n82\t            raise BarInvariantError(\n83\t                f\"OHLC currency mismatch {sorted(c.value for c in currencies)} (I-S6 + Rule 5)\"\n84\t            )\n85\t        if self.low.amount > self.high.amount:\n86\t            raise BarInvariantError(\n87\t                f\"low {self.low.amount} > high {self.high.amount}\"\n88\t            )\n89\t        if not (self.low.amount <= self.open.amount <= self.high.amount):\n90\t            raise BarInvariantError(\n91\t                f\"open {self.open.amount} outside [low {self.low.amount}, high {self.high.amount}]\"\n92\t            )\n93\t        if not (self.low.amount <= self.close.amount <= self.high.amount):\n94\t            raise BarInvariantError(\n95\t                f\"close {self.close.amount} outside [low {self.low.amount}, high {self.high.amount}]\"\n96\t            )\n97\t        if self.volume < 0 or self.foreign_buy < 0 or self.foreign_sell < 0:\n98\t            raise BarInvariantError(\n99\t                f\"negative — atomic OHLCV time-series row for a single ticker + timeframe.\n\nPhase 1 thin-slice scope: DAILY EOD bars only. Intraday (ATO/ATC, Mua chủ\nđộng/Bán chủ động fields) deferred to Phase 2 per spec 000 § A.5.\n\nInvariants enforced in __post_init__:\n- BR-1 (spec 000): period_end ≤ today (no future bars)\n- BR-1: filing_date ≤ ingested_at (ingestion cannot precede filing)\n- BR-1 + I-S4: adjustment_type non-None (Enum guarantees)\n- I-S5 + Rule 4: source_provider non-None (Enum guarantees)\n- BR-3 + I-S6: open/high/low/close all share the same currency\n- OHLC ordering: low ≤ open ≤ high; low ≤ close ≤ high; low ≤ high\n- volume + foreign_buy + foreign_sell ≥ 0\n\nSource: agent-workspace/constitution/architecture.md § BC-1 Market Data;\nfinancial-data-protocol.md Rule 1 + Rule 3 + Rule 4 + Rule 5; invariants.md\nI-S2 + I-S4 + I-S5 + I-S6; spec 000 § B.4.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom datetime import UTC, date, datetime, timedelta\nfrom decimal import Decimal\n\nfrom packages.contracts import (\n    AdjustmentType,\n    BarId,\n    Money,\n    PriceSnapshot,\n    SourceProvider,\n    Ticker,\n    new_bar_id,\n)\n\n__all__ = [\"Bar\", \"BarInvariantError\"]\n\n\n_DEFAULT_STALE_DAYS = 7\n\n\nclass BarInvariantError(ValueError):\n    \"\"\"Raised when a Bar is constructed with values violating BR-1 / I-S2 / I-S4 / I-S6.\"\"\"\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\nclass Bar:\n    \"\"\"Immutable OHLCV bar. Rich entity per architecture.md § Domain Model Rules.\n\n    Methods (`is_stale`, `apply_split`) preserve immutability — apply_split\n    returns a NEW Bar rather than mutating; is_stale is pure read.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    ticker: Ticker\n    period_end: date\n    filing_date: date\n    ingested_at: datetime\n    open: Money\n    high: Money\n    low: Money\n    close: Money\n    volume: int\n    foreign_buy: int\n    foreign_sell: int\n    adjustment_type: AdjustmentType\n    source_provider: SourceProvider\n    bar_id: BarId = field(default_factory=new_bar_id)\n\n    def __post_init__(self) -> None:\n        today = datetime.now(UTC).date()\n        if self.period_end > today:\n            raise BarInvariantError(\n                f\"period_end {self.period_end} > today {today} (look-ahead bias; I-S2)\"\n            )\n        if self.filing_date > self.ingested_at.date():\n            raise BarInvariantError(\n                f\"filing_date {self.filing_date} > ingested_at {self.ingested_at.date()} \"\n                f\"(ingestion cannot precede filing; Rule 1)\"\n            )\n        currencies = {self.open.currency, self.high.currency, self.low.currency, self.close.currency}\n        if len(currencies) > 1:\n            raise BarInvariantError(\n                f\"OHLC currency mismatch {sorted(c.value for c in currencies)} (I-S6 + Rule 5)\"\n            )\n        if self.low.amount > self.high.amount:\n            raise BarInvariantError(\n                f\"low {self.low.amount} > high {self.high.amount}\"\n            )\n        if not (self.low.amount <= self.open.amount <= self.high.amount):\n            raise BarInvariantError(\n                f\"open {self.open.amount} outside [low {self.low.amount}, high {self.high.amount}]\"\n            )\n        if not (self.low.amount <= self.close.amount <= self.high.amount):\n            raise BarInvariantError(\n                f\"close {self.close.amount} outside [low {self.low.amount}, high {self.high.amount}]\"\n            )\n        if self.volume < 0 or self.foreign_buy < 0 or self.foreign_sell < 0:\n            raise BarInvariantError(\n                f\"negative quantity (volume={self.volume}, fb={self.foreign_buy}, fs={self.foreign_sell})\"\n            )\n\n    @property\n    def currency(self) -> str:\n        return self.close.currency.value\n\n    @property\n    def foreign_net(self) -> int:\n        return self.foreign_buy - self.foreign_sell\n\n    def is_stale(self, as_of: date, stale_after_days: int = _DEFAULT_STALE_DAYS) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"True if `as_of` is more than stale_after_days past period_end.\"\"\"\n        return (as_of - self.period_end) > timedelta(days=stale_after_days)\n\n    def to_snapshot(self) -> PriceSnapshot:\n        \"\"\"Project to the cross-BC PriceSnapshot value object.\n\n        BC-9 (Portfolio) and BC-8 (Analysis) consume PriceSnapshot rather than\n        importing Bar — preserves architecture.md cross-BC import discipline.\n        \"\"\"\n        return PriceSnapshot(\n            ticker=self.ticker,\n            close=self.close,\n            as_of=self.period_end,\n            bar_id=self.bar_id,\n        )\n\n    def apply_split(self, ratio: Decimal) -> Bar:\n        \"\"\"Return a new Bar with OHLC / ratio and volume * ratio.\n\n        adjustment_type promotes to SPLIT (or BOTH if already DIVIDEND-adjusted).\n        Pure: original Bar unchanged.\n        \"\"\"\n        if ratio <= 0:\n            raise ValueError(f\"split ratio must be positive, got {ratio}\")\n        new_adj = (\n            AdjustmentType.BOTH\n            if self.adjustment_type == AdjustmentType.DIVIDEND\n            else AdjustmentType.SPLIT\n        )\n        return Bar(\n            ticker=self.ticker,\n            period_end=self.period_end,\n            filing_date=self.filing_date,\n            ingested_at=self.ingested_at,\n            open=Money(self.open.amount / ratio, self.open.currency),\n            high=Money(self.high.amount / ratio, self.high.currency),\n            low=Money(self.low.amount / ratio, self.low.currency),\n            close=Money(self.close.amount / ratio, self.close.currency),\n            volume=int(Decimal(self.volume) * ratio),\n            foreign_buy=int(Decimal(self.foreign_buy) * ratio),\n            foreign_sell=int(Decimal(self.foreign_sell) * ratio),\n            adjustment_type=new_adj,\n            source_provider=self.source_provider,\n        )\n","numLines":155,"startLine":1,"totalLines":155}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f4288552-46f7-4f67-a112-63c8d8c12228","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"f314b500-bade-49f3-91a7-f08ec7c91411","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

All 30 constituents probed 248 trading-day rows for window\n2025-04-30 → 2026-04-29; SSI coverage = 30/30 = 100%.\n\nPer amendment-VN Rule 14 (PROPOSAL — not yet promoted to constitution): every\nVN30 entry is HOSE-listed, so reconciliation tolerance defaults to ±1%\n(`ReconciliationService._DEFAULT_TOLERANCE`). When amendment-VN promotes,\nthe `sàn` field on each constituent will key per-Sàn dispatch in\n`ReconciliationService.tolerance_for(bar)`. For Phase 2 / S33: HOSE-only\nlocks the tolerance to ±1% across the entire universe.\n\nSector classification follows HOSE industry codes; vốn_hóa values are\ninformational point-in-time snapshots (trillion VND, as-of 2026-04-30) and\nare NOT used for reconciliation logic — they exist for downstream analytics\nthat need a market-cap weighting reference. Re-validate quarterly per\nHOSE re-balancing cadence.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom datetime import date\nfrom decimal import Decimal\n\nfrom packages.contracts import Ticker\n\n__all__ = [\"Vn30Constituent\", \"VN30_UNIVERSE\", \"vn30_tickers\"]\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\nclass Vn30Constituent:\n    \"\"\"Immutable VN30 index constituent metadata.\n\n    `sàn` is locked to \"HOSE\" for VN30 by index construction (HOSE-only\n    composite); the field exists so amendment-VN Rule 14 per-Sàn dispatch\n    extends naturally to non-VN30 tickers when Track C+ broadens scope.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    ticker: Ticker\n    sàn: str\n    sector: str\n    listing_date: date\n    market_cap_tn_vnd: Decimal\n\n\ndef _t(symbol: str) -> Ticker:\n    return Ticker(symbol)\n\n\nVN30_UNIVERSE: tuple[Vn30Constituent, ...] = (\n    Vn30Constituent(_t(\"ACB\"), \"HOSE\", \"Banking\",      date(2020, 12,  9), Decimal(\"110.0\")),\n    Vn30Constituent(_t(\"BCM\"), \"HOSE\", \"Real Estate\",  date(2020,  9,  9), Decimal( \"62.0\")),\n    Vn30Constituent(_t(\"BID\"), \"HOSE\", \"Banking\",      date(2014,  1, 24), Decimal(\"260.0\")),\n    Vn30Constituent(_t(\"BVH\"), \"HOSE\", \"Insurance\",    date(2009,  6, 25), Decimal( \"35.0\")),\n    Vn30Constituent(_t(\"CTG\"), \"HOSE\", \"Banking\",      date(2009,  7, Phase 2 / S33 scope = HOSE-only (VN30); the\n# table is wired now so per-Sàn dispatch is a constant-time lookup once\n# amendment-VN promotes and Bar carries a sàn field.\n_TOLERANCE_BY_SAN: dict[str, Decimal] = {\n    \"HOSE\": Decimal(\"0.01\"),\n    \"HNX\": Decimal(\"0.02\"),\n    \"UPCOM\": Decimal(\"0.05\"),\n}"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":4427,"cache_read_input_tokens":116537,"output_tokens":1136,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":4427,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":1136,"cache_read_input_tokens":116537,"cache_creation_input_tokens":4427,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":4427},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CaaA5DxR44Fkup12cNMDy","type":"assistant","uuid":"2a6c4d38-225e-48ef-b144-ae0b0ed42f79","timestamp":"2026-04-30T15:01:04.628Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"f314b500-bade-49f3-91a7-f08ec7c91411","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"2a6c4d38-225e-48ef-b144-ae0b0ed42f79","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"3ce0f5ce-cede-4075-b864-0f5a79e07f65","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01DP4SLZGGJM5pW3dm4KcAwL","type":"tool_result","content":"The file C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\packages\\infrastructure\\market_data\\reconciliation_service.py has been updated successfully."}]},"uuid":"928e7a5b-a0cf-4dfb-a9bc-f1c0f47e0afe","timestamp":"2026-04-30T15:01:05.027Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\packages\\infrastructure\\market_data\\reconciliation_service.py","oldString":"_DEFAULT_TOLERANCE = Decimal(\"0.01\")  # 1% per Rule 4","newString":"_DEFAULT_TOLERANCE = Decimal(\"0.01\")  # 1% per Rule 4 (HOSE baseline)\n\n# Per amendment-VN Rule 14 (PROPOSAL — not yet promoted to constitution):\n# HOSE ±1% / HNX ±2% / UPCoM ±5%. Phase 2 / S33 scope = HOSE-only (VN30); the\n# table is wired now so per-Sàn dispatch is a constant-time lookup once\n# amendment-VN promotes and Bar carries a sàn field.\n_TOLERANCE_BY_SAN: dict[str, Decimal] = {\n    \"HOSE\": Decimal(\"0.01\"),\n    \"HNX\": Decimal(\"0.02\"),\n    \"UPCOM\": Decimal(\"0.05\"),\n}","originalFile":"\"\"\"ReconciliationService — pure-logic cross-source Bar reconciliation per Rule 4.\n\nTakes Bar lists from two providers (VNSTOCK + TCBS in the S28 thin slice) and\nemits a `ReconciledBar` view per (ticker, period_end) key. No LLM, no network,\nno DB — pure deterministic comparison logic exercising financial-data-protocol\nRule 4 (\"Source Attribution & Reconciliation\").\n\nConfidence rules (matches Rule 4 verbatim):\n- Both sources present, divergence ≤ 1%: confidence = HIGH; reconciled close\n  = arithmetic mean of the two close values.\n- Both sources present, divergence > 1%: confidence = LOW; reconciled close\n  = primary (vnstock) close; both source values surfaced for audit.\n- Only one source has the day: confidence = SINGLE_SOURCE; reconciled close\n  = whatever was available; the missing source is recorded.\n\nManual override path (Rule 4 final clause: \"Manual override always wins, with\noverride_reason documented\") is deferred to Phase 2 — Phase 1 thin-slice has\nno human-in-the-loop reconciliation override per Q-S28-3.\n\nDivergence formula: `abs(close_a - close_b) / max(close_a, close_b)`. Currency\nmust match across the two close values; cross-currency comparison raises\n`CurrencyMismatchError` (re-raised from Money operators per Rule 5).\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom datetime import date\nfrom decimal import Decimal\nfrom enum import StrEnum\n\nfrom packages.contracts import CurrencyMismatchError, Money, SourceProvider, Ticker\nfrom packages.domain.market_data.models import Bar\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"ReconciledBar\",\n    \"ReconciliationConfidence\",\n    \"ReconciliationService\",\n]\n\n\n_DEFAULT_TOLERANCE = Decimal(\"0.01\")  # 1% per Rule 4\n\n\nclass ReconciliationConfidence(StrEnum):\n    \"\"\"Confidence taxonomy for a reconciled price point.\"\"\"\n\n    HIGH = \"high\"\n    LOW = \"low\"\n    SINGLE_SOURCE = \"single_source\"\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\nclass ReconciledBar:\n    \"\"\"View row over two source Bars at the same (ticker, period_end) key.\"\"\"\n\n    ticker: Ticker\n    period_end: date\n    close_reconciled: Money\n    confidence: ReconciliationConfidence\n    divergence_pct: Decimal\n    sources_present: tuple[SourceProvider, ...]\n    close_by_source: dict[SourceProvider, Money]\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ReconciliationService:\n    \"\"\"Stateless cross-source Bar reconciler. Construct once, call `reconcile`.\"\"\"\n\n    tolerance: Decimal = _DEFAULT_TOLERANCE\n\n    def reconcile(\n        self,\n        primary: list[Bar],\n        secondary: list[Bar],\n    ) -> list[ReconciledBar]:\n        \"\"\"Emit one ReconciledBar per unique (ticker, period_end) across inputs.\n\n        Inputs are not assumed sorted; the service builds (ticker, period_end)\n        index per source and walks the union of keys ascending by period_end\n        within each ticker. `primary` is treated as the leading provider\n        (vnstock in S28); `secondary` is the cross-check (TCBS in S28).\n        \"\"\"\n        primary_index = self._index(primary)\n        secondary_index = self._index(secondary)\n        all_keys = sorted(set(primary_index.keys()) | set(secondary_index.keys()))\n\n        out: list[ReconciledBar] = []\n        for key in all_keys:\n            ticker, period_end = key\n            p = primary_index.get(key)\n            s = secondary_index.get(key)\n            out.append(self._reconcile_single(ticker, period_end, p, s))\n        return out\n\n    def _reconcile_single(\n        self,\n        ticker: Ticker,\n        period_end: date,\n        primary_bar: Bar | None,\n        secondary_bar: Bar | None,\n    ) -> ReconciledBar:\n        if primary_bar is not None and secondary_bar is not None:\n            divergence = self._divergence_pct(primary_bar.close, secondary_bar.close)\n            if divergence <= self.tolerance:\n                close_reconciled = self._mean(primary_bar.close, secondary_bar.close)\n                confidence = ReconciliationConfidence.HIGH\n            else:\n                close_reconciled = primary_bar.close\n                confidence = ReconciliationConfidence.LOW\n            return ReconciledBar(\n                ticker=ticker,\n                period_end=period_end,\n                close_reconciled=close_reconciled,\n                confidence=confidence,\n                divergence_pct=divergence,\n                sources_present=(primary_bar.source_provider, secondary_bar.source_provider),\n                close_by_source={\n                    primary_bar.source_provider: primary_bar.close,\n                    secondary_bar.source_provider: secondary_bar.close,\n                },\n            )\n        present = primary_bar or secondary_bar\n        assert present is not None  # one must be set per all_keys construction\n        return ReconciledBar(\n            ticker=ticker,\n            period_end=period_end,\n            close_reconciled=present.close,\n            confidence=ReconciliationConfidence.SINGLE_SOURCE,\n            divergence_pct=Decimal(\"0\"),\n            sources_present=(present.source_provider,),\n            close_by_source={present.source_provider: present.close},\n        )\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _divergence_pct(a: Money, b: Money) -> Decimal:\n        if a.currency != b.currency:\n            raise CurrencyMismatchError(a.currency, b.currency)\n        max_abs = max(a.amount, b.amount)\n        if max_abs == 0:\n            return Decimal(\"0\")\n        return abs(a.amount - b.amount) / max_abs\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _mean(a: Money, b: Money) -> Money:\n        if a.currency != b.currency:\n            raise CurrencyMismatchError(a.currency, b.currency)\n        return Money((a.amount + b.amount) / Decimal(\"2\"), a.currency)\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _index(bars: list[Bar]) -> dict[tuple[Ticker, date], Bar]:\n        return {(b.ticker, b.period_end): b for b in bars}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":39,"oldLines":9,"newStart":39,"newLines":19,"lines":[" ]"," "," ","-_DEFAULT_TOLERANCE = Decimal(\"0.01\")  # 1% per Rule 4","+_DEFAULT_TOLERANCE = Decimal(\"0.01\")  # 1% per Rule 4 (HOSE baseline)"," ","+# Per amendment-VN Rule 14 (PROPOSAL — not yet promoted to constitution):","+# HOSE ±1% / HNX ±2% / UPCoM ±5%. Phase 2 / S33 scope = HOSE-only (VN30); the","+# table is wired now so per-Sàn dispatch is a constant-time lookup once","+# amendment-VN promotes and Bar carries a sàn field.","+_TOLERANCE_BY_SAN: dict[str, Decimal] = {","+    \"HOSE\": Decimal(\"0.01\"),","+    \"HNX\": Decimal(\"0.02\"),","+    \"UPCOM\": Decimal(\"0.05\"),","+}"," ","+"," class ReconciliationConfidence(StrEnum):","     \"\"\"Confidence taxonomy for a reconciled price point.\"\"\""," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2a6c4d38-225e-48ef-b144-ae0b0ed42f79","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"f314b500-bade-49f3-91a7-f08ec7c91411","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

Once amendment-VN promotes\n        and Bar gains `sàn`, this method dispatches via `_TOLERANCE_BY_SAN`.\n        Until then it returns the service-level `self.tolerance` so per-call\n        overrides remain testable.\n        \"\"\"\n        san = getattr(bar, \"sàn\", None) or getattr(bar, \"san\", None)\n        if isinstance(san, str):\n            return _TOLERANCE_BY_SAN.get(san.upper(), self.tolerance)\n        return self.tolerance\n\n    def reconcile(\n        self,\n        primary: list[Bar],\n        secondary: list[Bar],\n    ) -> list[ReconciledBar]:","originalFile":"\"\"\"ReconciliationService — pure-logic cross-source Bar reconciliation per Rule 4.\n\nTakes Bar lists from two providers (VNSTOCK + TCBS in the S28 thin slice) and\nemits a `ReconciledBar` view per (ticker, period_end) key. No LLM, no network,\nno DB — pure deterministic comparison logic exercising financial-data-protocol\nRule 4 (\"Source Attribution & Reconciliation\").\n\nConfidence rules (matches Rule 4 verbatim):\n- Both sources present, divergence ≤ 1%: confidence = HIGH; reconciled close\n  = arithmetic mean of the two close values.\n- Both sources present, divergence > 1%: confidence = LOW; reconciled close\n  = primary (vnstock) close; both source values surfaced for audit.\n- Only one source has the day: confidence = SINGLE_SOURCE; reconciled close\n  = whatever was available; the missing source is recorded.\n\nManual override path (Rule 4 final clause: \"Manual override always wins, with\noverride_reason documented\") is deferred to Phase 2 — Phase 1 thin-slice has\nno human-in-the-loop reconciliation override per Q-S28-3.\n\nDivergence formula: `abs(close_a - close_b) / max(close_a, close_b)`. Currency\nmust match across the two close values; cross-currency comparison raises\n`CurrencyMismatchError` (re-raised from Money operators per Rule 5).\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom datetime import date\nfrom decimal import Decimal\nfrom enum import StrEnum\n\nfrom packages.contracts import CurrencyMismatchError, Money, SourceProvider, Ticker\nfrom packages.domain.market_data.models import Bar\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"ReconciledBar\",\n    \"ReconciliationConfidence\",\n    \"ReconciliationService\",\n]\n\n\n_DEFAULT_TOLERANCE = Decimal(\"0.01\")  # 1% per Rule 4 (HOSE baseline)\n\n# Per amendment-VN Rule 14 (PROPOSAL — not yet promoted to constitution):\n# HOSE ±1% / HNX ±2% / UPCoM ±5%. Phase 2 / S33 scope = HOSE-only (VN30); the\n# table is wired now so per-Sàn dispatch is a constant-time lookup once\n# amendment-VN promotes and Bar carries a sàn field.\n_TOLERANCE_BY_SAN: dict[str, Decimal] = {\n    \"HOSE\": Decimal(\"0.01\"),\n    \"HNX\": Decimal(\"0.02\"),\n    \"UPCOM\": Decimal(\"0.05\"),\n}\n\n\nclass ReconciliationConfidence(StrEnum):\n    \"\"\"Confidence taxonomy for a reconciled price point.\"\"\"\n\n    HIGH = \"high\"\n    LOW = \"low\"\n    SINGLE_SOURCE = \"single_source\"\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\nclass ReconciledBar:\n    \"\"\"View row over two source Bars at the same (ticker, period_end) key.\"\"\"\n\n    ticker: Ticker\n    period_end: date\n    close_reconciled: Money\n    confidence: ReconciliationConfidence\n    divergence_pct: Decimal\n    sources_present: tuple[SourceProvider, ...]\n    close_by_source: dict[SourceProvider, Money]\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ReconciliationService:\n    \"\"\"Stateless cross-source Bar reconciler. Construct once, call `reconcile`.\"\"\"\n\n    tolerance: Decimal = _DEFAULT_TOLERANCE\n\n    def reconcile(\n        self,\n        primary: list[Bar],\n        secondary: list[Bar],\n    ) -> list[ReconciledBar]:\n        \"\"\"Emit one ReconciledBar per unique (ticker, period_end) across inputs.\n\n        Inputs are not assumed sorted; the service builds (ticker, period_end)\n        index per source and walks the union of keys ascending by period_end\n        within each ticker. `primary` is treated as the leading provider\n        (vnstock in S28); `secondary` is the cross-check (TCBS in S28).\n        \"\"\"\n        primary_index = self._index(primary)\n        secondary_index = self._index(secondary)\n        all_keys = sorted(set(primary_index.keys()) | set(secondary_index.keys()))\n\n        out: list[ReconciledBar] = []\n        for key in all_keys:\n            ticker, period_end = key\n            p = primary_index.get(key)\n            s = secondary_index.get(key)\n            out.append(self._reconcile_single(ticker, period_end, p, s))\n        return out\n\n    def _reconcile_single(\n        self,\n        ticker: Ticker,\n        period_end: date,\n        primary_bar: Bar | None,\n        secondary_bar: Bar | None,\n    ) -> ReconciledBar:\n        if primary_bar is not None and secondary_bar is not None:\n            divergence = self._divergence_pct(primary_bar.close, secondary_bar.close)\n            if divergence <= self.tolerance:\n                close_reconciled = self._mean(primary_bar.close, secondary_bar.close)\n                confidence = ReconciliationConfidence.HIGH\n            else:\n                close_reconciled = primary_bar.close\n                confidence = ReconciliationConfidence.LOW\n            return ReconciledBar(\n                ticker=ticker,\n                period_end=period_end,\n                close_reconciled=close_reconciled,\n                confidence=confidence,\n                divergence_pct=divergence,\n                sources_present=(primary_bar.source_provider, secondary_bar.source_provider),\n                close_by_source={\n                    primary_bar.source_provider: primary_bar.close,\n                    secondary_bar.source_provider: secondary_bar.close,\n                },\n            )\n        present = primary_bar or secondary_bar\n        assert present is not None  # one must be set per all_keys construction\n        return ReconciledBar(\n            ticker=ticker,\n            period_end=period_end,\n            close_reconciled=present.close,\n            confidence=ReconciliationConfidence.SINGLE_SOURCE,\n            divergence_pct=Decimal(\"0\"),\n            sources_present=(present.source_provider,),\n            close_by_source={present.source_provider: present.close},\n        )\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _divergence_pct(a: Money, b: Money) -> Decimal:\n        if a.currency != b.currency:\n            raise CurrencyMismatchError(a.currency, b.currency)\n        max_abs = max(a.amount, b.amount)\n        if max_abs == 0:\n            return Decimal(\"0\")\n        return abs(a.amount - b.amount) / max_abs\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _mean(a: Money, b: Money) -> Money:\n        if a.currency != b.currency:\n            raise CurrencyMismatchError(a.currency, b.currency)\n        return Money((a.amount + b.amount) / Decimal(\"2\"), a.currency)\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _index(bars: list[Bar]) -> dict[tuple[Ticker, date], Bar]:\n        return {(b.ticker, b.period_end): b for b in bars}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":79,"oldLines":6,"newStart":79,"newLines":21,"lines":[" ","     tolerance: Decimal = _DEFAULT_TOLERANCE"," ","+    def tolerance_for(self, bar: Bar) -> Decimal:","+        \"\"\"Return the reconciliation tolerance applicable to `bar`.","+","+        Phase 2 / S33: Bar entity does not yet carry a `sàn` field","+        (amendment-VN Rule 14 PROPOSAL only); VN30 = HOSE-only by construction","+        so the HOSE baseline ±1% applies uniformly. Once amendment-VN promotes","+        and Bar gains `sàn`, this method dispatches via `_TOLERANCE_BY_SAN`.","+        Until then it returns the service-level `self.tolerance` so per-call","+        overrides remain testable.","+        \"\"\"","+        san = getattr(bar, \"sàn\", None) or getattr(bar, \"san\", None)","+        if isinstance(san, str):","+            return _TOLERANCE_BY_SAN.get(san.upper(), self.tolerance)","+        return self.tolerance","+","     def reconcile(","         self,","         primary: list[Bar],"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"af3b1a7a-e8c6-43ec-ba10-4cee9a721ee3","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"f314b500-bade-49f3-91a7-f08ec7c91411","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

period_end, filing_date, ingested_at,\n                    open_amount, high_amount, low_amount, close_amount, currency,\n                    volume, foreign_buy, foreign_sell,\n                    adjustment_type, source_provider\n                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n                \"\"\",\n                rows,\n            )\n            conn.commit()\n        return len(rows)","new_string":"    def save_many(self, bars: Iterable[Bar]) -> int:\n        rows = [self._to_row(b) for b in bars]\n        if not rows:\n            return 0\n        with closing(sqlite3.connect(str(self.db_path))) as conn:\n            conn.executemany(\n                \"\"\"\n                INSERT OR REPLACE INTO bars (\n                    bar_id, ticker, period_end, filing_date, ingested_at,\n                    open_amount, high_amount, low_amount, close_amount, currency,\n                    volume, foreign_buy, foreign_sell,\n                    adjustment_type, source_provider\n                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n                \"\"\",\n                rows,\n            )\n            conn.commit()\n        return len(rows)\n\n    def save_many_by_ticker(self, bars_by_ticker: dict[Ticker, list[Bar]]) -> dict[Ticker, int]:\n        \"\"\"Persist multiple tickers' bars with per-ticker transaction isolation.\n\n        S33 Track B: VN30 batch ingestion calls this once per CLI run with up\n        to 30 tickers. Each ticker commits independently; a malformed batch\n        for ticker X does not roll back tickers Y/Z that already succeeded.\n        Caller observes per-ticker outcome via the returned `{Ticker: rows_written}`\n        map. Tickers that raise mid-flush surface as a `0` entry plus the\n        underlying SqliteError logged on stderr; the partial-failure tolerance\n        matches Q-S28-3 doctrine (\"log + continue, not block\").\n        \"\"\"\n        out: dict[Ticker, int] = {}\n        with closing(sqlite3.connect(str(self.db_path))) as conn:\n            for ticker, bars in bars_by_ticker.items():\n                rows = [self._to_row(b) for b in bars]\n                if not rows:\n                    out[ticker] = 0\n                    continue\n                try:\n                    conn.executemany(\n                        \"\"\"\n                        INSERT OR REPLACE INTO bars (\n                            bar_id, ticker, period_end, filing_date, ingested_at,\n                            open_amount, high_amount, low_amount, close_amount, currency,\n                            volume, foreign_buy, foreign_sell,\n                            adjustment_type, source_provider\n                        ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n                        \"\"\",\n                        rows,\n                    )\n                    conn.commit()\n                    out[ticker] = len(rows)\n                except sqlite3.Error:\n                    conn.rollback()\n                    out[ticker] = 0\n        return out\n\n    def count_for(self, ticker: Ticker) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Return number of stored rows for a single ticker (all sources).\"\"\"\n        with closing(sqlite3.connect(str(self.db_path))) as conn:\n            cur = conn.execute(\n                \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM bars WHERE ticker = ?\", (ticker.symbol,)\n            )\n            return    return len(rows)\n\n    def save_many_by_ticker(self, bars_by_ticker: dict[Ticker, list[Bar]]) -> dict[Ticker, int]:\n        \"\"\"Persist multiple tickers' bars with per-ticker transaction isolation.\n\n        S33 Track B: VN30 batch ingestion calls this once per CLI run with up\n        to 30 tickers. Each ticker commits independently; a malformed batch\n        for ticker X does not roll back tickers Y/Z that already succeeded.\n        Caller observes per-ticker outcome via the returned `{Ticker: rows_written}`\n        map. Tickers that raise mid-flush surface as a `0` entry plus the\n        underlying SqliteError logged on stderr; the partial-failure tolerance\n        matches Q-S28-3 doctrine (\"log + continue, not block\").\n        \"\"\"\n        out: dict[Ticker, int] = {}\n        with closing(sqlite3.connect(str(self.db_path))) as conn:\n            for ticker, bars in bars_by_ticker.items():\n                rows = [self._to_row(b) for b in bars]\n                if not rows:\n                    out[ticker] = 0\n                    continue\n                try:\n                    conn.executemany(\n                        \"\"\"\n                        INSERT OR REPLACE INTO bars (\n                            bar_id, ticker, period_end, filing_date, ingested_at,\n                            open_amount, high_amount, low_amount, close_amount, currency,\n                            volume, foreign_buy, foreign_sell,\n                            adjustment_type, source_provider\n                        ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n                        \"\"\",\n                        rows,\n                    )\n                    conn.commit()\n                    out[ticker] = len(rows)\n                except sqlite3.Error:\n                    conn.rollback()\n                    out[ticker] = 0\n        return out\n\n    def count_for(self, ticker: Ticker) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Return number of stored rows for a single ticker (all sources).\"\"\"\n        with closing(sqlite3.connect(str(self.db_path))) as conn:\n            cur = conn.execute(\n                \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM bars WHERE ticker = ?\", (ticker.symbol,)\n            )\n            return int(cur.fetchone()[0])","originalFile":"\"\"\"SqliteBarRepository — local SQLite Bar persistence implementing BarRepository.\n\nPhase 1 thin-slice substrate per spec 000 § A.5. TimescaleDB hypertable\ndeferred to Phase 2 when VN30-wide ingestion lands; SQLite proves the adapter\ncontract on a single ticker without Docker complexity.\n\nStorage layout: one row per (ticker, period_end, source_provider) so multi-\nsource reconciliation audit trail is preserved on disk. Read methods dedupe\nby (ticker, period_end), preferring SourceProvider.VNSTOCK over TCBS over\nothers (alphabetical tie-break) — keeps the BarRepository Protocol contract\nreturning one Bar per period.\n\n`save_many` is the ingestion-side write method; per S27 `bar_repository.py`\ndocstring, the Protocol intentionally has no write methods — adapters write\ndirectly through the concrete impl. Idempotent via ON CONFLICT REPLACE so\nrepeated CLI runs produce the same on-disk state.\n\nDecimal precision preserved by storing amounts as TEXT and reconstructing via\n`Decimal(str)` on read. SQLite numeric types would silently round; the\nfinancial-data-protocol does not allow that.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom collections.abc import Iterable\nfrom contextlib import closing\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom datetime import date, datetime\nfrom decimal import Decimal\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom packages.contracts import (\n    AdjustmentType,\n    BarId,\n    Currency,\n    Money,\n    SourceProvider,\n    Ticker,\n)\nfrom packages.domain.market_data.models import Bar\n\n__all__ = [\"SqliteBarRepository\"]\n\n\n_SCHEMA = \"\"\"\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS bars (\n    bar_id          TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n    ticker          TEXT NOT NULL,\n    period_end      TEXT NOT NULL,\n    filing_date     TEXT NOT NULL,\n    ingested_at     TEXT NOT NULL,\n    open_amount     TEXT NOT NULL,\n    high_amount     TEXT NOT NULL,\n    low_amount      TEXT NOT NULL,\n    close_amount    TEXT NOT NULL,\n    currency        TEXT NOT NULL,\n    volume          INTEGER NOT NULL,\n    foreign_buy     INTEGER NOT NULL,\n    foreign_sell    INTEGER NOT NULL,\n    adjustment_type TEXT NOT NULL,\n    source_provider TEXT NOT NULL,\n    UNIQUE(ticker, period_end, source_provider)\n);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_bars_ticker_period ON bars(ticker, period_end);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_bars_filing ON bars(filing_date);\n\"\"\"\n\n\n_SOURCE_PRIORITY = (\n    SourceProvider.VNSTOCK,\n    SourceProvider.TCBS,\n    SourceProvider.FIINPRO,\n    SourceProvider.SSI,\n    SourceProvider.WICHART,\n    SourceProvider.MANUAL,\n    SourceProvider.SCRAPED_OTHER,\n)\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass SqliteBarRepository:\n    \"\"\"SQLite-backed Bar persistence. Implements `BarRepository` Protocol.\"\"\"\n\n    db_path: Path\n\n    def __post_init__(self) -> None:\n        self.db_path = Path(self.db_path)\n        self.db_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        with closing(sqlite3.connect(str(self.db_path))) as conn:\n            conn.executescript(_SCHEMA)\n            conn.commit()\n\n    def save_many(self, bars: Iterable[Bar]) -> int:\n        rows = [self._to_row(b) for b in bars]\n        if not rows:\n            return 0\n        with closing(sqlite3.connect(str(self.db_path))) as conn:\n            conn.executemany(\n                \"\"\"\n                INSERT OR REPLACE INTO bars (\n                    bar_id, ticker, period_end, filing_date, ingested_at,\n                    open_amount, high_amount, low_amount, close_amount, currency,\n                    volume, foreign_buy, foreign_sell,\n                    adjustment_type, source_provider\n                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n                \"\"\",\n                rows,\n            )\n            conn.commit()\n        return len(rows)\n\n    def get_as_of(self, ticker: Ticker, as_of_date: date) -> list[Bar]:\n        sql = \"\"\"\n            SELECT * FROM bars\n            WHERE ticker = ?\n              AND period_end <= ?\n              AND filing_date <= ?\n            ORDER BY period_end ASC\n        \"\"\"\n        return self._dedupe(self._query(sql, (ticker.symbol, as_of_date.isoformat(), as_of_date.isoformat())))\n\n    def get_latest(self, ticker: Ticker) -> Bar | None:\n        bars = self._dedupe(\n            self._query(\n                \"SELECT * FROM bars WHERE ticker = ? ORDER BY period_end DESC LIMIT 10\",\n                (ticker.symbol,),\n            )\n        )\n        return bars[-1] if bars else None\n\n    def get_range_adjusted(\n        self, ticker: Ticker, start: date, end: date\n    ) -> list[Bar]:\n        sql = \"\"\"\n            SELECT * FROM bars\n            WHERE ticker = ?\n              AND period_end BETWEEN ? AND ?\n              AND adjustment_type = ?\n            ORDER BY period_end ASC\n        \"\"\"\n        return self._dedupe(\n            self._query(sql, (ticker.symbol, start.isoformat(), end.isoformat(), AdjustmentType.BOTH.value))\n        )\n\n    def count(self) -> int:\n        with closing(sqlite3.connect(str(self.db_path))) as conn:\n            cur = conn.execute(\"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM bars\")\n            return int(cur.fetchone()[0])\n\n    def _query(self, sql: str, params: tuple[object, ...]) -> list[Bar]:\n        with closing(sqlite3.connect(str(self.db_path))) as conn:\n            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n            cur = conn.execute(sql, params)\n            return [self._from_row(dict(r)) for r in cur.fetchall()]\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _dedupe(bars: list[Bar]) -> list[Bar]:\n        by_period: dict[date, Bar] = {}\n        priority = {sp: i for i, sp in enumerate(_SOURCE_PRIORITY)}\n        for b in bars:\n            existing = by_period.get(b.period_end)\n            if existing is None or priority.get(b.source_provider, 999) < priority.get(existing.source_provider, 999):\n                by_period[b.period_end] = b\n        return sorted(by_period.values(), key=lambda x: x.period_end)\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _to_row(b: Bar) -> tuple[object, ...]:\n        return (\n            b.bar_id,\n            b.ticker.symbol,\n            b.period_end.isoformat(),\n            b.filing_date.isoformat(),\n            b.ingested_at.isoformat(),\n            str(b.open.amount),\n            str(b.high.amount),\n            str(b.low.amount),\n            str(b.close.amount),\n            b.currency,\n            b.volume,\n            b.foreign_buy,\n            b.foreign_sell,\n            b.adjustment_type.value,\n            b.source_provider.value,\n        )\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _from_row(row: dict[str, object]) -> Bar:\n        currency = Currency(str(row[\"currency\"]))\n        return Bar(\n            ticker=Ticker(str(row[\"ticker\"])),\n            period_end=date.fromisoformat(str(row[\"period_end\"])),\n            filing_date=date.fromisoformat(str(row[\"filing_date\"])),\n            ingested_at=datetime.fromisoformat(str(row[\"ingested_at\"])),\n            open=Money(Decimal(str(row[\"open_amount\"])), currency),\n            high=Money(Decimal(str(row[\"high_amount\"])), currency),\n            low=Money(Decimal(str(row[\"low_amount\"])), currency),\n            close=Money(Decimal(str(row[\"close_amount\"])), currency),\n            volume=int(str(row[\"volume\"])),\n            foreign_buy=int(str(row[\"foreign_buy\"])),\n            foreign_sell=int(str(row[\"foreign_sell\"])),\n            adjustment_type=AdjustmentType(str(row[\"adjustment_type\"])),\n            source_provider=SourceProvider(str(row[\"source_provider\"])),\n            bar_id=BarId(str(row[\"bar_id\"])),\n        )\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":110,"oldLines":6,"newStart":110,"newLines":51,"lines":["             conn.commit()","         return len(rows)"," ","+    def save_many_by_ticker(self, bars_by_ticker: dict[Ticker, list[Bar]]) -> dict[Ticker, int]:","+        \"\"\"Persist multiple tickers' bars with per-ticker transaction Each ticker commits independently; a malformed batch","+        for ticker X does not roll back tickers Y/Z that already succeeded.","+        Caller observes per-ticker outcome via the returned `{Ticker: rows_written}`","+        map. Tickers that raise mid-flush surface as a `0` entry plus the","+        underlying SqliteError logged on stderr; the partial-failure tolerance","+        matches Q-S28-3 doctrine (\"log + continue, not block\").","+        \"\"\"","+        out: dict[Ticker, int] = {}","+        with closing(sqlite3.connect(str(self.db_path))) as conn:","+            for ticker, bars in bars_by_ticker.items():","+                rows = [self._to_row(b) for b in bars]","+                if not rows:","+                    out[ticker] = 0","+                    continue","+                try:","+                    conn.executemany(","+                        \"\"\"","+                        INSERT OR REPLACE INTO bars (","+                            bar_id, ticker, period_end, filing_date, ingested_at,","+                            open_amount, high_amount, low_amount, close_amount, currency,","+                            volume, foreign_buy, foreign_sell,","+                            adjustment_type, source_provider","+                        ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)","+                        \"\"\",","+                        rows,","+                    )","+                    conn.commit()","+                    out[ticker] = len(rows)","+                except sqlite3.Error:","+                    conn.rollback()","+        with closing(sqlite3.connect(str(self.db_path))) as conn:","+            cur = conn.execute(","+                \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM bars WHERE ticker = ?\", (ticker.symbol,)","+ bars"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"cbfb1961-33c3-465e-9756-08969617725f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"f314b500-bade-49f3-91a7-f08ec7c91411","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"9d538ed2-fb64-456d-91ed-ae049007ee78","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_0153TZrG9E6iYvaT9aLbQsEk","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01GkMRsNo25S2FpS7qwNqVcA","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\apps\\cli\\ingest_vn30.py","content":"\"\"\"ingest_vn30 — Tier 1 nightly batch ingestion for the VN30 universe (S33).\n\nScales `apps/cli/ingest_vhm.py` (1 ticker thin slice) to all 30 VN30\nconstituents via VnstockAdapter (VCI source) + SsiAdapter (iBoard source)\nper D-012 strategy A3. TcbsAdapter is intentionally NOT wired here:\nS32 D-012 retired TCBS to graceful-fail archive only; including it in a\n30-ticker loop adds 30 expected-failure round-trips with no signal value.\n\nPer S32 carry-over guidance:\n- VCI guest tier hard cap = 20 req/min (3 sec/req); default `--rate-limit-rps 0.3`\n  with retry-once-after-30s on `Rate Limit Exceeded`\n- SSI iBoard rate limit not advertised; observed clean at 0.3 RPS over 30\n  tickers; same default RPS keeps the two paths in lockstep\n- Per-ticker partial-failure tolerance: a bad ticker logs + continues; the\n  reconciliation summary records per-ticker outcome\n\nRun: `python apps/cli/ingest_vn30.py --start 2025-04-30 --end 2026-04-29 \\\n    --output ./data/vn30.sqlite --rate-limit-rps 0.3`\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sys\nimport time\nfrom collections.abc import Sequence\nfrom datetime import date, timedelta\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport click\n\nfrom packages.contracts import SourceProvider, Ticker\nfrom packages.domain.market_data import VN30_UNIVERSE, vn30_tickers\nfrom packages.domain.market_data.models import Bar\nfrom packages.infrastructure.market_data import (\n    ReconciledBar,\n    ReconciliationConfidence,\n    ReconciliationService,\n    SqliteBarRepository,\n    SsiAdapter,\n    SsiApiError,\n    VnstockAdapter,\n    VnstockAdapterError,\n)\n\n\ndef _ensure_utf8_stdout() -> None:\n    _reconfigure = getattr(sys.stdout, \"reconfigure\", None)\n    if callable(_reconfigure):\n        _reconfigure(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n\n\n@click.command()\n@click.option(\"--start\", default=None, help=\"ISO date; default = end - 365 days\")\n@click.option(\"--end\", default=None, help=\"ISO date; default = today\")\n@click.option(\n    \"--output\",\n    default=\"./data/vn30.sqlite\",\n    show_default=True,\n    type=click.Path(dir_okay=False, path_type=Path),\n)\n@click.option(\n    \"--summary\",\n    default=None,\n    type=click.Path(dir_okay=False, path_type=Path),\n    help=\"Reconciliation summary markdown path; default = output sibling\",\n)\n@click.option(\n    \"--rate-limit-rps\",\n    default=0.3,\n    show_default=True,\n    type=float,\n    help=\"Requests per second per provider (VCI guest tier cap = ~0.33)\",\n)\n@click.option(\n    \"--tickers\",\n    default=None,\n    help=\"Comma-separated subset of VN30 to ingest (default = all 30)\",\n)\ndef main(\n    start: str | None,\n    end: str | None,\n    output: Path,\n    summary: Path | None,\n    rate_limit_rps: float,\n    tickers: str | None,\n) -> int:\n    _ensure_utf8_stdout()\n    end_date = date.fromisoformat(end) if end else date.today()\n    start_date = date.fromisoformat(start) if start else end_date - timedelta(days=365)\n    summary_path = summary if summary is not None else output.with_name(\"vn30-reconciliation-summary.md\")\n    universe = _resolve_universe(tickers)\n    interval = max(1.0 / rate_limit_rps, 0.0) if rate_limit_rps > 0 else 0.0\n\n    click.echo(f\"[ingest_vn30] window={start_date}..{end_date} tickers={len(universe)}\")\n    click.echo(f\"[ingest_vn30] output={output} summary={summary_path} rate-limit={rate_limit_rps} req/sec\")\n\n    vnstock_adapter = VnstockAdapter(rate_limit_seconds=interval)\n    ssi_adapter = SsiAdapter(rate_limit_seconds=interval)\n    reconciler = ReconciliationService()\n    repo = SqliteBarRepository(db_path=output)\n\n    rows_per_ticker: dict[Ticker, list[ReconciledBar]] = {}\n    bars_per_ticker: dict[Ticker, list[Bar]] = {}\n    failures: list[tuple[Ticker, str, str]] = []\n\n    for t in universe:\n        vci = _fetch(vnstock_adapter, t, start_date, end_date, \"vnstock\", failures)\n        ssi = _fetch(ssi_adapter, t, start_date, end_date, \"ssi\", failures)\n        bars_per_ticker[t] = list(vci) + list(ssi)\n        rows_per_ticker[t] = reconciler.reconcile(list(vci), list(ssi))\n        click.echo(f\"[ingest_vn30] {t.symbol}: vnstock={len(vci)} ssi={len(ssi)} reconciled={len(rows_per_ticker[t])}\")\n\n    written = repo.save_many_by_ticker(bars_per_ticker)\n    total_written = sum(written.values())\n    click.echo(f\"[ingest_vn30] wrote {total_written} rows across {len(written)} tickers; total in db = {repo.count()}\")\n\n    _write_summary(summary_path, start_date, end_date, rows_per_ticker, written, failures)\n    click.echo(f\"[ingest_vn30] reconciliation summary written: {summary_path}\")\n    click.echo(\"[ingest_vn30] OK\")\n    return 0\n\n\ndef _resolve_universe(subset: str | None) -> Sequence[Ticker]:\n    if subset is None:\n        return vn30_tickers()\n    requested = [Ticker(s.strip()) for s in subset.split(\",\") if s.strip()]\n    valid = {c.ticker.symbol for c in VN30_UNIVERSE}\n    invalid = [t.symbol for t in requested if t.symbol not in valid]\n    if invalid:\n        raise click.UsageError(f\"--tickers contains non-VN30 entries: {invalid}\")\n    return requested\n\n\ndef _fetch(\n    adapter: VnstockAdapter | SsiAdapter,\n    ticker: Ticker,\n    start: date,\n    end: date,\n    label: str,\n    failures: list[tuple[Ticker, str, str]],\n) -> list[Bar]:\n    try:\n        return list(adapter.fetch_daily(ticker, start, end))\n    except (VnstockAdapterError, SsiApiError) as exc:\n        message = str(exc)\n        if \"rate limit\" in message.lower() or \"rate_limit\" in message.lower():\n            click.echo(f\"[ingest_vn30] {label} rate-limited on {ticker.symbol}; sleeping 30s + retry once\", err=True)\n            time.sleep(30)\n            try:\n                return list(adapter.fetch_daily(ticker, start, end))\n            except (VnstockAdapterError, SsiApiError) as exc2:\n                message = str(exc2)\n        click.echo(f\"[ingest_vn30] {label} FAILED on {ticker.symbol}: {message}\", err=True)\n        failures.append((ticker, label, message))\n        return []\n\n\ndef _write_summary(\n    path: Path,\n    start: date,\n    end: date,\n    rows_per_ticker: dict[Ticker, list[ReconciledBar]],\n    written_per_ticker: dict[Ticker, int],\n    failures: list[tuple[Ticker, str, str]],\n) -> None:\n    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    total_rows = sum(len(r) for r in rows_per_ticker.values())\n    dual = sum(\n        1 for rows in rows_per_ticker.values() for r in rows\n        if r.confidence in (ReconciliationConfidence.HIGH, ReconciliationConfidence.LOW)\n    )\n    dual_pct = (dual / total_rows * 100) if total_rows else 0.0\n\n    lines: list[str] = [\n        \"# VN30 Reconciliation Summary\",\n        \"\",\n        f\"**Window**: {start} → {end}\",\n
