TcbsAdapter is intentionally NOT wired here:\nS32 D-012 retired TCBS to graceful-fail archive only; including it in a\n30-ticker loop adds 30 expected-failure round-trips with no signal value.\n\nPer S32 carry-over guidance:\n- VCI guest tier hard cap = 20 req/min (3 sec/req); default `--rate-limit-rps 0.3`\n  with retry-once-after-30s on `Rate Limit Exceeded`\n- SSI iBoard rate limit not advertised; observed clean at 0.3 RPS over 30\n  tickers; same default RPS keeps the two paths in lockstep\n- Per-ticker partial-failure tolerance: a bad ticker logs + continues; the\n  reconciliation summary records per-ticker outcome\n\nRun: `python apps/cli/ingest_vn30.py --start 2025-04-30 --end 2026-04-29 \\\n    --output ./data/vn30.sqlite --rate-limit-rps 0.3`\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sys\nimport time\nfrom collections.abc import Sequence\nfrom datetime import date, timedelta\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport click\n\nfrom packages.contracts import SourceProvider, Ticker\nfrom packages.domain.market_data import VN30_UNIVERSE, vn30_tickers\nfrom packages.domain.market_data.models import Bar\nfrom packages.infrastructure.market_data import (\n    ReconciledBar,\n    ReconciliationConfidence,\n    ReconciliationService,\n    SqliteBarRepository,\n    SsiAdapter,\n    SsiApiError,\n    VnstockAdapter,\n    VnstockAdapterError,\n)\n\n\ndef _ensure_utf8_stdout() -> None:\n    _reconfigure = getattr(sys.stdout, \"reconfigure\", None)\n    if callable(_reconfigure):\n        _reconfigure(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n\n\n@click.command()\n@click.option(\"--start\", default=None, help=\"ISO date; default = end - 365 days\")\n@click.option(\"--end\", default=None, help=\"ISO date; default = today\")\n@click.option(\n    \"--output\",\n    default=\"./data/vn30.sqlite\",\n    show_default=True,\n    type=click.Path(dir_okay=False, path_type=Path),\n)\n@click.option(\n    \"--summary\",\n    default=None,\n    type=click.Path(dir_okay=False, path_type=Path),\n    help=\"Reconciliation summary markdown path; default = output sibling\",\n)\n@click.option(\n    \"--rate-limit-rps\",\n    default=0.3,\n    show_default=True,\n    type=float,\n    help=\"Requests per second per provider (VCI guest tier cap = ~0.33)\",\n)\n@click.option(\n    \"--tickers\",\n    default=None,\n    help=\"Comma-separated subset of VN30 to ingest (default = all 30)\",\n)\ndef main(\n    start: str | None,\n    end: str | None,\n    output: Path,\n    summary: Path | None,\n    rate_limit_rps: float,\n    tickers: str | None,\n) -> int:\n    _ensure_utf8_stdout()\n    end_date = date.fromisoformat(end) if end else date.today()\n    start_date = date.fromisoformat(start) if start else end_date - timedelta(days=365)\n    summary_path = summary if summary is not None else output.with_name(\"vn30-reconciliation-summary.md\")\n    universe = _resolve_universe(tickers)\n    interval = max(1.0 / rate_limit_rps, 0.0) if rate_limit_rps > 0 else 0.0\n\n    click.echo(f\"[ingest_vn30] window={start_date}..{end_date} tickers={len(universe)}\")\n    click.echo(f\"[ingest_vn30] output={output} summary={summary_path} rate-limit={rate_limit_rps} req/sec\")\n\n    vnstock_adapter = VnstockAdapter(rate_limit_seconds=interval)\n    ssi_adapter = SsiAdapter(rate_limit_seconds=interval)\n    reconciler = ReconciliationService()\n    repo = SqliteBarRepository(db_path=output)\n\n    rows_per_ticker: dict[Ticker, list[ReconciledBar]] = {}\n    bars_per_ticker: dict[Ticker, list[Bar]] = {}\n    failures: list[tuple[Ticker, str, str]] = []\n\n    for t in universe:\n        vci = _fetch(vnstock_adapter, t, start_date, end_date, \"vnstock\", failures)\n        ssi = _fetch(ssi_adapter, t, start_date, end_date, \"ssi\", failures)\n        bars_per_ticker[t] = list(vci) + list(ssi)\n        rows_per_ticker[t] = reconciler.reconcile(list(vci), list(ssi))\n        click.echo(f\"[ingest_vn30] {t.symbol}: vnstock={len(vci)} ssi={len(ssi)} reconciled={len(rows_per_ticker[t])}\")\n\n    written = repo.save_many_by_ticker(bars_per_ticker)\n    total_written = sum(written.values())\n    click.echo(f\"[ingest_vn30] wrote {total_written} rows across {len(written)} tickers; total in db = {repo.count()}\")\n\n    _write_summary(summary_path, start_date, end_date, rows_per_ticker, written, failures)\n    click.echo(f\"[ingest_vn30] reconciliation summary written: {summary_path}\")\n    click.echo(\"[ingest_vn30] OK\")\n    return 0\n\n\ndef _resolve_universe(subset: str | None) -> Sequence[Ticker]:\n    if subset is None:\n        return vn30_tickers()\n    requested = [Ticker(s.strip()) for s in subset.split(\",\") if s.strip()]\n    valid = {c.ticker.symbol for c in VN30_UNIVERSE}\n    invalid = [t.symbol for t in requested if t.symbol not in valid]\n    if invalid:\n        raise click.UsageError(f\"--tickers contains non-VN30 entries: {invalid}\")\n    return requested\n\n\ndef _fetch(\n    adapter: VnstockAdapter | SsiAdapter,\n    ticker: Ticker,\n    start: date,\n    end: date,\n    label: str,\n    failures: list[tuple[Ticker, str, str]],\n) -> list[Bar]:\n    try:\n        return list(adapter.fetch_daily(ticker, start, end))\n    except (VnstockAdapterError, SsiApiError) as exc:\n        message = str(exc)\n        if \"rate limit\" in message.lower() or \"rate_limit\" in message.lower():\n            click.echo(f\"[ingest_vn30] {label} rate-limited on {ticker.symbol}; sleeping 30s + retry once\", err=True)\n            time.sleep(30)\n            try:\n                return list(adapter.fetch_daily(ticker, start, end))\n            except (VnstockAdapterError, SsiApiError) as exc2:\n                message = str(exc2)\n        click.echo(f\"[ingest_vn30] {label} FAILED on {ticker.symbol}: {message}\", err=True)\n        failures.append((ticker, label, message))\n        return []\n\n\ndef _write_summary(\n    path: Path,\n    start: date,\n    end: date,\n    rows_per_ticker: dict[Ticker, list[ReconciledBar]],\n    written_per_ticker: dict[Ticker, int],\n    failures: list[tuple[Ticker, str, str]],\n) -> None:\n    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    total_rows = sum(len(r) for r in rows_per_ticker.values())\n    dual = sum(\n        1 for rows in rows_per_ticker.values() for r in rows\n        if r.confidence in (ReconciliationConfidence.HIGH, ReconciliationConfidence.LOW)\n    )\n    dual_pct = (dual / total_rows * 100) if total_rows else 0.0\n\n    lines: list[str] = [\n        \"# VN30 Reconciliation Summary\",\n        \"\",\n        f\"**Window**: {start} → {end}\",\n        f\"**Tickers ingested**: {len(rows_per_ticker)} / 30\",\n        f\"**Total reconciled rows**: {total_rows}\",\n        f\"**Dual-source rows**: {dual} ({dual_pct:.1f}%)\",\n        \"\",\n        \"## Per-ticker outcome\",\n        \"\",\n        \"| Ticker | rows_written | vnstock_rows | ssi_rows | dual | low | single | confidence |\",\n        \"|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\",\n    ]\n    for ticker, rows in rows_per_ticker.items():\n        n_dual = sum(1 for r in rows if r.confidence == ReconciliationConfidence.HIGH)\n        n_low = sum(1 for r in rows if r.confidence == ReconciliationConfidence.LOW)\n        n_single = sum(1 for r in rows if r.confidence == ReconciliationConfidence.SINGLE_SOURCE)\n        vci_count = sum(1 for r in rows if SourceProvider.VNSTOCK in r.sources_present)\n        ssi_count = sum(1 for r in rows if SourceProvider.SSI in r.sources_present)\n        verdict = \"DUAL_SOURCE\" if (n_dual + n_low) >= n_single else \"SINGLE_SOURCE\"\n        lines.append(\n            f\"| {ticker.symbol} | {written_per_ticker.get(ticker, 0)} | {vci_count} | {ssi_count} \"\n            f\"| {n_dual} | {n_low} | {n_single} | {verdict} |\"\n        )\n    if failures:\n        lines += [\"\", \"## Failures\", \"\"]\n        for t, lbl, msg in failures:\n            lines.append(f\"- **{t.symbol}** [{lbl}]: {msg}\")\n    path.write_text(\"\\n\".join(lines) + \"\\n\", encoding=\"utf-8\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main(standalone_mode=False) or 0)\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"d26d8e2e-4227-4413-9022-bc0aa5b9170f","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"f314b500-bade-49f3-91a7-f08ec7c91411","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"cf031cda-0d24-4d07-a28a-0df870487dbe","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_019KmB4ffYeHz6JFyY9LCtoe","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01JMZ2CiuARs2V4JEyEDrMiK","name":"Edit","input":{"replace_all":false,"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\packages\\infrastructure\\market_data\\test_adapters.py","old_string":"    def test_decimal_precision_preserved(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n        repo = self._repo(tmp_path)\n        precise = _bar(period_end=date(2026, 4, 28), close_amount=\"123456.789\")\n        repo.save_many([precise])\n        latest = repo.get_latest(Ticker(\"VHM\"))\n        assert latest is not None\n        assert latest.close.amount == Decimal(\"123456.789\")","new_string":"    def test_decimal_precision_preserved(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n        repo = self._repo(tmp_path)\n        precise = _bar(period_end=date(2026, 4, 28), close_amount=\"123456.789\")\n        repo.save_many([precise])\n        latest = repo.get_latest(Ticker(\"VHM\"))\n        assert latest is not None\n        assert latest.close.amount == Decimal(\"123456.789\")\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# VN30 universe tests (S33 Track B — BC-1 expansion)\n\n\nclass TestVn30Universe:\n    \"\"\"Verify the frozen VN30 universe value object meets index invariants.\"\"\"\n\n    def test_exactly_30_constituents(self) -> None:\n        from packages.domain.market_data import VN30_UNIVERSE\n        assert len(VN30_UNIVERSE) == 30\n\n    def test_all_tickers_unique(self) -> None:\n        from packages.domain.market_data import VN30_UNIVERSE\n        symbols = [c.ticker.symbol for c in VN30_UNIVERSE]\n        assert len(set(symbols)) == 30\n\n    def test_all_constituents_HOSE(self) -> None:\n        from packages.domain.market_data import VN30_UNIVERSE\n        # VN30 is HOSE-only by index construction (amendment-VN Rule 14 S33 lock)\n        assert all(c.sàn == \"HOSE\" for c in VN30_UNIVERSE)\n\n    def test_vn30_tickers_helper_returns_alphabetical_order(self) -> None:\n        from packages.domain.market_data import vn30_tickers\n        tickers = vn30_tickers()\n        symbols = [t.symbol for t in tickers]\n        assert symbols == sorted(symbols), \"VN30 should canonicalise alphabetically\"\n\n    def test_known_anchor_tickers_present(self) -> None:\n        # Sanity-check anchor blue chips that have been in VN30 since 2018\n        # (cross-checked against data/vn30-probe-ssi.json S32 30/30 probe)\n        from packages.domain.market_data import vn30_tickers\n        symbols = {t.symbol for t in vn30_tickers()}\n        for anchor in (\"VHM\", \"VIC\", \"VCB\", \"FPT\", \"HPG\", \"VPB\"):\n            assert anchor in symbols, f\"VN30 anchor {anchor} missing\"\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Sàn-tier tolerance tests (S33 Track B — amendment-VN Rule 14 wiring)\n\n\nclass TestSanTierTolerance:\n    def test_default_tolerance_when_bar_has_no_san_field(self) -> None:\n        # Bar entity does not yet carry sàn (amendment-VN PROPOSAL); should\n        # fall back to service.tolerance (1% HOSE baseline).\n        svc = ReconciliationService()\n        b = _bar(period_end=date(2026, 4, 28))\n        assert svc.tolerance_for(b) == Decimal(\"0.01\")\n\n    def test_custom_service_tolerance_propagates(self) -> None:\n        svc = ReconciliationService(tolerance=Decimal(\"0.005\"))\n        b = _bar(period_end=date(2026, 4, 28))\n        assert svc.tolerance_for(b) == Decimal(\"0.005\")\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Multi-ticker batched insert tests (S33 Track B — VN30 nightly batch)\n\n\nclass TestMultiTickerBatchedInsert:\n    def _repo(self, tmp_path: Path) -> SqliteBarRepository:\n        return SqliteBarRepository(db_path=tmp_path / \"vn30.sqlite\")\n\n    def test_save_many_by_ticker_persists_each_ticker(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n        repo = self._repo(tmp_path)\n        d = date(2026, 4, 28)\n        bars_by_ticker = {\n            Ticker(\"VHM\"): [_bar(ticker=\"VHM\", period_end=d)],\n            Ticker(\"VIC\"): [_bar(ticker=\"VIC\", period_end=d)],\n            Ticker(\"FPT\"): [_bar(ticker=\"FPT\", period_end=d)],\n        }\n        result = repo.save_many_by_ticker(bars_by_ticker)\n        assert result == {Ticker(\"VHM\"): 1, Ticker(\"VIC\"): 1, Ticker(\"FPT\"): 1}\n        assert repo.count() == 3\n\n    def test_save_many_by_ticker_empty_ticker_returns_zero(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n        repo = self._repo(tmp_path)\n        result = repo.save_many_by_ticker({Ticker(\"VHM\"): []})\n        assert result == {Ticker(\"VHM\"): 0}\n        assert repo.count() == 0\n\n    def test_count_for_filters_by_ticker(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n        repo = self._repo(tmp_path)\n        d1 = date(2026, 4, 28)\n        d2 = date(2026, 4, 29)\n        repo.save_many_by_ticker({\n            Ticker(\"VHM\"): [_bar(ticker=\"VHM\", period_end=d1), _bar(ticker=\"VHM\", period_end=d2)],\n            Ticker(\"VIC\"): [_bar(ticker=\"VIC\", period_end=d1)],\n        })\n        assert repo.count_for(Ticker(\"VHM\")) == 2\n        assert repo.count_for(Ticker(\"VIC\")) == 1\n        assert repo.count_for(Ticker(\"FPT\")) == 0  # not ingested\n\n    def test_save_many_by_ticker_per_ticker_isolation(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n        # Partial-failure tolerance: success on one ticker should persist even\n        # if a separate call would have failed.\n        repo = self._repo(tmp_path)\n        d = date(2026, 4, 28)\n        result = repo.save_many_by_ticker({\n            Ticker(\"VHM\"): [_bar(ticker=\"VHM\", period_end=d)],\n            Ticker(\"VIC\"): [],\n        })\n        assert result[Ticker(\"VHM\")] == 1\n        assert result[Ticker(\"VIC\")] == 0\n        assert repo.count_for(Ticker(\"VHM\")) == 1"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":921,"cache_read_input_tokens":128558,"output_tokens":2461,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":921,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":2461,"cache_read_input_tokens":128558,"cache_creation_input_tokens":921,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":921},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CaaABhLNU6TfbD7Ek7X8h","type":"assistant","uuid":"19a7aae4-24cc-4bab-85c2-414d1d637f2f","timestamp":"2026-04-30T15:02:48.976Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"f314b500-bade-49f3-91a7-f08ec7c91411","version":"2.1.123","gitBranch":"main"}

None:","+        from packages.domain.market_data import VN30_UNIVERSE","+        assert len(VN30_UNIVERSE) == 30","+","+    def test_all_tickers_unique(self) -> None:","+        from packages.domain.market_data import VN30_UNIVERSE","+        symbols = [c.ticker.symbol for c in VN30_UNIVERSE]","+        assert len(set(symbols)) == 30","+","+    def test_all_constituents_HOSE(self) -> None:","+        from packages.domain.market_data import VN30_UNIVERSE","+        # VN30 is HOSE-only by index construction (amendment-VN Rule 14 S33 lock)","+        assert all(c.sàn == \"HOSE\" for c in VN30_UNIVERSE)","+","+    def test_vn30_tickers_helper_returns_alphabetical_order(self) -> None:","+        from packages.domain.market_data import vn30_tickers","+        tickers = vn30_tickers()","+        symbols = [t.symbol for t in tickers]","+        assert symbols == sorted(symbols), \"VN30 should canonicalise alphabetically\"","+","+    def test_known_anchor_tickers_present(self) -> None:","+        # Sanity-check anchor blue chips that have been in VN30 since 2018","+        # (cross-checked against data/vn30-probe-ssi.json S32 30/30 probe)","+        from packages.domain.market_data import vn30_tickers","+        symbols = {t.symbol for t in vn30_tickers()}","+        for anchor in (\"VHM\", \"VIC\", \"VCB\", \"FPT\", \"HPG\", \"VPB\"):","+            assert anchor in symbols, f\"VN30 anchor {anchor} missing\"","+","+","+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------","+# Sàn-tier tolerance tests (S33 Track B — amendment-VN Rule 14 wiring)","+","+","+class TestSanTierTolerance:","+    def test_default_tolerance_when_bar_has_no_san_field(self) -> None:","+        # Bar entity does not yet carry sàn (amendment-VN PROPOSAL); should","+        # fall back to service.tolerance (1% HOSE baseline).","+        svc = ReconciliationService()","+        b = _bar(period_end=date(2026, 4, 28))","+        assert svc.tolerance_for(b) == Decimal(\"0.01\")","+","+    def test_custom_service_tolerance_propagates(self) -> None:","+        svc = ReconciliationService(tolerance=Decimal(\"0.005\"))","+        b = _bar(period_end=date(2026, 4, 28))","+        assert svc.tolerance_for(b) == Decimal(\"0.005\")","+","+","+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------","+# Multi-ticker batched insert tests (S33 Track B — VN30 nightly batch)","+","+","+class TestMultiTickerBatchedInsert:","+    def _repo(self, tmp_path: Path) -> SqliteBarRepository:","+        return SqliteBarRepository(db_path=tmp_path / \"vn30.sqlite\")","+","+    def test_save_many_by_ticker_persists_each_ticker(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:","+        repo = self._repo(tmp_path)","+        d = date(2026, 4, 28)","+        bars_by_ticker = {","+            Ticker(\"VHM\"): [_bar(ticker=\"VHM\", period_end=d)],","+            Ticker(\"VIC\"): [_bar(ticker=\"VIC\", period_end=d)],","+            Ticker(\"FPT\"): [_bar(ticker=\"FPT\", period_end=d)],","+        }","+        result = repo.save_many_by_ticker(bars_by_ticker)","+        assert result == {Ticker(\"VHM\"): 1, Ticker(\"VIC\"): 1, Ticker(\"FPT\"): 1}","+        assert repo.count() == 3","+","+    def test_save_many_by_ticker_empty_ticker_returns_zero(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:","+        repo = self._repo(tmp_path)","+        result = repo.save_many_by_ticker({Ticker(\"VHM\"): []})","+        assert result == {Ticker(\"VHM\"): 0}","+        assert repo.count() == 0","+","+    def test_count_for_filters_by_ticker(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:","+        repo = self._repo(tmp_path)","+        d1 = date(2026, 4, 28)","+        d2 = date(2026, 4, 29)","+        repo.save_many_by_ticker({","+            Ticker(\"VHM\"): [_bar(ticker=\"VHM\", period_end=d1), _bar(ticker=\"VHM\", period_end=d2)],","+            Ticker(\"VIC\"): [_bar(ticker=\"VIC\", period_end=d1)],","+        })","+        assert repo.count_for(Ticker(\"VHM\")) == 2","+        assert repo.count_for(Ticker(\"VIC\")) == 1","+        assert repo.count_for(Ticker(\"FPT\")) == 0  # not ingested","+","+    def test_save_many_by_ticker_per_ticker_isolation(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:","+        # Partial-failure tolerance: success on one ticker should persist even","+        # if a separate call would have failed.","+        repo = self._repo(tmp_path)","+        d = date(2026, 4, 28)","+        result = repo.save_many_by_ticker({","+            Ticker(\"VHM\"): [_bar(ticker=\"VHM\", period_end=d)],","+            Ticker(\"VIC\"): [],","+        })","+        assert result[Ticker(\"VHM\")] == 1","+        assert result[Ticker(\"VIC\")] == 0","+        assert repo.count_for(Ticker(\"VHM\")) == fetch + reconcile,\nSQLite write, summary markdown emission.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom datetime import UTC, date, datetime\nfrom decimal import Decimal\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\nfrom click.testing import CliRunner\n\nfrom packages.contracts import (\n    AdjustmentType,\n    Currency,\n    Money,\n    SourceProvider,\n    Ticker,\n)\nfrom packages.domain.market_data.models import Bar\n\n\ndef _bar(*, ticker: str, period_end: date, source: SourceProvider, close: str = \"100000\") -> Bar:\n    return Bar(\n        ticker=Ticker(ticker),\n        period_end=period_end,\n        filing_date=period_end,\n        ingested_at=datetime(2026, 4, 30, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC),\n        open=Money(Decimal(close), Currency.VND),\n        high=Money(Decimal(close) + Decimal(\"500\"), Currency.VND),\n        low=Money(Decimal(close) - Decimal(\"500\"), Currency.VND),\n        close=Money(Decimal(close), Currency.VND),\n        volume=1_000_000,\n        foreign_buy=0,\n        foreign_sell=0,\n        adjustment_type=AdjustmentType.BOTH,\n        source_provider=source,\n    )\n\n\n@pytest.fixture()\ndef stub_adapters(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Monkey-patch VnstockAdapter + SsiAdapter to return canned Bar lists.\"\"\"\n    from apps.cli import ingest_vn30 as cli_mod\n\n    def vnstock_fetch(self: object, ticker: Ticker, start: date, end: date) -> list[Bar]:\n        return [_bar(ticker=ticker.symbol, period_end=date(2026, 4, 28), source=SourceProvider.VNSTOCK)]\n\n    def ssi_fetch(self: object, ticker: Ticker, start: date, end: date) -> list[Bar]:\n        return [_bar(ticker=ticker.symbol, period_end=date(2026, 4, 28), source=SourceProvider.SSI, close=\"100200\")]\n\n    monkeypatch.setattr(cli_mod.VnstockAdapter, \"fetch_daily\", vnstock_fetch)\n    monkeypatch.setattr(cli_mod.SsiAdapter, \"fetch_daily\", ssi_fetch)\n    monkeypatch.setattr(cli_mod.VnstockAdapter, \"__post_init__\", lambda self: None)\n    monkeypatch.setattr(cli_mod.SsiAdapter, \"__post_init__\", lambda self: None)\n\n\ndef test_subset_run_writes_per_ticker_rows(stub_adapters: None, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n    from apps.cli.ingest_vn30 import main\n    runner = CliRunner()\n    db = tmp_path / \"vn30.sqlite\"\n    summary = tmp_path / \"summary.md\"\n    result = runner.invoke(\n        main,\n        [\n            \"--start\", \"2026-04-01\",\n            \"--end\", \"2026-04-29\",\n            \"--output\", str(db),\n            \"--summary\", str(summary),\n            \"--rate-limit-rps\", \"1000\",\n            \"--tickers\", \"VHM,VIC,FPT\",\n        ],\n        standalone_mode=False,\n    )\n    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.output\n    assert \"vn30] OK\" in result.output\n    assert db.exists()\n    assert summary.exists()\n\n\ndef test_subset_run_emits_summary_markdown(stub_adapters: None, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n    from apps.cli.ingest_vn30 import main\n    runner = CliRunner()\n    db = tmp_path / \"vn30.sqlite\"\n    summary = tmp_path / \"summary.md\"\n    runner.invoke(\n        main,\n        [\n            \"--start\", \"2026-04-01\",\n            \"--end\", \"2026-04-29\",\n            \"--output\", str(db),\n            \"--summary\", str(summary),\n            \"--rate-limit-rps\", \"1000\",\n            \"--tickers\", \"VHM,VIC\",\n        ],\n        standalone_mode=False,\n    )\n    body = summary.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    assert \"# VN30 Reconciliation Summary\" in body\n    assert \"VHM\" in body\n    assert \"VIC\" in body\n    assert \"DUAL_SOURCE\" in body  # both stub adapters return rows\n\n\ndef test_summary_reports_dual_source_when_both_adapters_return(stub_adapters: None, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n    from apps.cli.ingest_vn30 import main\n    runner = CliRunner()\n    summary = tmp_path / \"summary.md\"\n    runner.invoke(\n        main,\n        [\n            \"--start\", \"2026-04-01\",\n            \"--end\", \"2026-04-29\",\n            \"--output\", str(tmp_path / \"vn30.sqlite\"),\n            \"--summary\", str(summary),\n            \"--rate-limit-rps\", \"1000\",\n            \"--tickers\", \"VHM\",\n        ],\n        standalone_mode=False,\n    )\n    body = summary.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    # 100000 vs 100200 = 0.2% divergence → HIGH confidence dual\n    assert \"100.0%\" in body or \"Dual-source rows: 1 (100.0%)\" in body\n\n\ndef test_invalid_ticker_subset_raises(stub_adapters: None, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n    from apps.cli.ingest_vn30 import main\n    runner = CliRunner()\n    result = runner.invoke(\n        main,\n        [\n            \"--output\", str(tmp_path / \"vn30.sqlite\"),\n            \"--tickers\", \"VHM,XXX\",  # XXX not in VN30\n        ],\n assert \"non-VN30\" in str(exc)\n\n\ndef test_default_universe_is_30_tickers() -> None:\n    from packages.domain.market_data import vn30_tickers\n    assert len(vn30_tickers()) == lambda _self: None)","originalFile":"\"\"\"CLI smoke tests for `apps/cli/ingest_vn30.py` (S33 Track B).\n\nNO live API calls — adapters are monkey-patched to return recorded Bar lists.\nVerifies CLI orchestration: universe resolution, per-ticker fetch + reconcile,\nSQLite write, summary markdown emission.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom datetime import UTC, date, datetime\nfrom decimal import Decimal\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\nfrom click.testing import CliRunner\n\nfrom packages.contracts import (\n    AdjustmentType,\n    Currency,\n    Money,\n    SourceProvider,\n    Ticker,\n)\nfrom packages.domain.market_data.models import Bar\n\n\ndef _bar(*, ticker: str, period_end: date, source: SourceProvider, close: str = \"100000\") -> Bar:\n    return Bar(\n        ticker=Ticker(ticker),\n        period_end=period_end,\n        filing_date=period_end,\n        ingested_at=datetime(2026, 4, 30, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC),\n        open=Money(Decimal(close), Currency.VND),\n        high=Money(Decimal(close) + Decimal(\"500\"), Currency.VND),\n        low=Money(Decimal(close) - Decimal(\"500\"), Currency.VND),\n        close=Money(Decimal(close), Currency.VND),\n        volume=1_000_000,\n        foreign_buy=0,\n        foreign_sell=0,\n        adjustment_type=AdjustmentType.BOTH,\n        source_provider=source,\n    )\n\n\n@pytest.fixture()\ndef stub_adapters(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Monkey-patch VnstockAdapter + SsiAdapter to return canned Bar lists.\"\"\"\n    from apps.cli import ingest_vn30 as cli_mod\n\n    def vnstock_fetch(self: object, ticker: Ticker, start: date, end: date) -> list[Bar]:\n        return [_bar(ticker=ticker.symbol, period_end=date(2026, 4, 28), source=SourceProvider.VNSTOCK)]\n\n    def ssi_fetch(self: object, ticker: Ticker, start: date, end: date) -> list[Bar]:\n        return [_bar(ticker=ticker.symbol, period_end=date(2026, 4, 28), source=SourceProvider.SSI, close=\"100200\")]\n\n    monkeypatch.setattr(cli_mod.VnstockAdapter, \"fetch_daily\", vnstock_fetch)\n    monkeypatch.setattr(cli_mod.SsiAdapter, \"fetch_daily\", ssi_fetch)\n    monkeypatch.setattr(cli_mod.VnstockAdapter, \"__post_init__\", lambda self: None)\n    monkeypatch.setattr(cli_mod.SsiAdapter, \"__post_init__\", lambda self: None)\n\n\ndef test_subset_run_writes_per_ticker_rows(stub_adapters: None, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n    from apps.cli.ingest_vn30 import main\n    runner = CliRunner()\n    db = tmp_path / \"vn30.sqlite\"\n    summary = tmp_path / \"summary.md\"\n    result = runner.invoke(\n        main,\n        [\n            \"--start\", \"2026-04-01\",\n            \"--end\", \"2026-04-29\",\n            \"--output\", str(db),\n            \"--summary\", str(summary),\n            \"--rate-limit-rps\", \"1000\",\n            \"--tickers\", \"VHM,VIC,FPT\",\n        ],\n        standalone_mode=False,\n    )\n    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.output\n    assert \"vn30] OK\" in result.output\n    assert db.exists()\n    assert summary.exists()\n\n\ndef test_subset_run_emits_summary_markdown(stub_adapters: None, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n    from apps.cli.ingest_vn30 import main\n    runner = CliRunner()\n    db = tmp_path / \"vn30.sqlite\"\n    summary = tmp_path / \"summary.md\"\n    runner.invoke(\n        main,\n        [\n            \"--start\", \"2026-04-01\",\n            \"--end\", \"2026-04-29\",\n            \"--output\", str(db),\n            \"--summary\", str(summary),\n            \"--rate-limit-rps\", \"1000\",\n            \"--tickers\", \"VHM,VIC\",\n        ],\n        standalone_mode=False,\n    )\n    body = summary.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    assert \"# VN30 Reconciliation Summary\" in body\n    assert \"VHM\" in body\n    assert \"VIC\" in body\n    assert \"DUAL_SOURCE\" in body  # both stub adapters return rows\n\n\ndef test_summary_reports_dual_source_when_both_adapters_return(stub_adapters: None, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n    from apps.cli.ingest_vn30 import main\n    runner = CliRunner()\n    summary = tmp_path / \"summary.md\"\n    runner.invoke(\n        main,\n        [\n            \"--start\", \"2026-04-01\",\n            \"--end\", \"2026-04-29\",\n            \"--output\", str(tmp_path / \"vn30.sqlite\"),\n            \"--summary\", str(summary),\n            \"--rate-limit-rps\", \"1000\",\n            \"--tickers\", \"VHM\",\n        ],\n        standalone_mode=False,\n    )\n    body = summary.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    # 100000 vs 100200 = 0.2% divergence → HIGH confidence dual\n    assert \"100.0%\" in body or \"Dual-source rows: 1 (100.0%)\" in body\n\n\ndef test_invalid_ticker_subset_raises(stub_adapters: None, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n    from apps.cli.ingest_vn30 import main\n    runner = CliRunner()\n    result = runner.invoke(\n        main,\n        [\n            \"--output\", str(tmp_path / \"vn30.sqlite\"),\n            \"--tickers\", \"VHM,XXX\",  # XXX not in VN30\n        ],\n        standalone_mode=False,\n    )\n    # UsageError surfaces as non-zero exit + message in result.exception\n    assert result.exit_code != 0\n    exc = result.exception\n    assert exc is not None\n    assert \"non-VN30\" in str(exc)\n\n\ndef test_default_universe_is_30_tickers() -> None:\n    from packages.domain.market_data import vn30_tickers\n    assert len(vn30_tickers()) == 30\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":45,"oldLines":18,"newStart":45,"newLines":18,"lines":[" @pytest.fixture()"," def stub_adapters(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:","     \"\"\"Monkey-patch None, tmp_path: Path) -> None:  # noqa: ARG001","originalFile":"\"\"\"CLI smoke tests for `apps/cli/ingest_vn30.py` (S33 Track B).\n\nNO live API calls — adapters are monkey-patched to return recorded Bar lists.\nVerifies CLI orchestration: universe resolution, per-ticker fetch + reconcile,\nSQLite write, summary markdown emission.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom datetime import UTC, date, datetime\nfrom decimal import Decimal\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\nfrom click.testing import CliRunner\n\nfrom packages.contracts import (\n    AdjustmentType,\n    Currency,\n    Money,\n    SourceProvider,\n    Ticker,\n)\nfrom packages.domain.market_data.models import Bar\n\n\ndef _bar(*, ticker: str, period_end: date, source: SourceProvider, close: str = \"100000\") -> Bar:\n    return Bar(\n        ticker=Ticker(ticker),\n        period_end=period_end,\n        filing_date=period_end,\n        ingested_at=datetime(2026, 4, 30, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC),\n        open=Money(Decimal(close), Currency.VND),\n        high=Money(Decimal(close) + Decimal(\"500\"), Currency.VND),\n        low=Money(Decimal(close) - Decimal(\"500\"), Currency.VND),\n        close=Money(Decimal(close), Currency.VND),\n        volume=1_000_000,\n        foreign_buy=0,\n        foreign_sell=0,\n        adjustment_type=AdjustmentType.BOTH,\n        source_provider=source,\n    )\n\n\n@pytest.fixture()\ndef stub_adapters(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Monkey-patch VnstockAdapter + SsiAdapter to return canned Bar lists.\"\"\"\n    from packages.infrastructure.market_data import SsiAdapter, VnstockAdapter\n\n    def vnstock_fetch(_self: object, ticker: Ticker, _start: date, _end: date) -> list[Bar]:\n        return [_bar(ticker=ticker.symbol, period_end=date(2026, 4, 28), source=SourceProvider.VNSTOCK)]\n\n    def ssi_fetch(_self: object, ticker: Ticker, _start: date, _end: date) -> list[Bar]:\n        return [_bar(ticker=ticker.symbol, period_end=date(2026, 4, 28), source=SourceProvider.SSI, close=\"100200\")]\n\n    monkeypatch.setattr(VnstockAdapter, \"fetch_daily\", vnstock_fetch)\n    monkeypatch.setattr(SsiAdapter, \"fetch_daily\", ssi_fetch)\n    monkeypatch.setattr(VnstockAdapter, \"__post_init__\", lambda _self: None)\n    monkeypatch.setattr(SsiAdapter, \"__post_init__\", lambda _self: None)\n\n\ndef test_subset_run_writes_per_ticker_rows(stub_adapters: None, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n    from apps.cli.ingest_vn30 import main\n    runner = CliRunner()\n    db = tmp_path / \"vn30.sqlite\"\n    summary = tmp_path / \"summary.md\"\n    result = runner.invoke(\n        main,\n        [\n            \"--start\", \"2026-04-01\",\n            \"--end\", \"2026-04-29\",\n            \"--output\", str(db),\n            \"--summary\", str(summary),\n            \"--rate-limit-rps\", \"1000\",\n            \"--tickers\", \"VHM,VIC,FPT\",\n        ],\n        standalone_mode=False,\n    )\n    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.output\n    assert \"vn30] OK\" in result.output\n    assert db.exists()\n    assert summary.exists()\n\n\ndef test_subset_run_emits_summary_markdown(stub_adapters: None, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n    from apps.cli.ingest_vn30 import main\n    runner = CliRunner()\n    db = tmp_path / \"vn30.sqlite\"\n    summary = tmp_path / \"summary.md\"\n    runner.invoke(\n        main,\n        [\n            \"--start\", \"2026-04-01\",\n            \"--end\", \"2026-04-29\",\n            \"--output\", str(db),\n            \"--summary\", str(summary),\n            \"--rate-limit-rps\", \"1000\",\n            \"--tickers\", \"VHM,VIC\",\n        ],\n        standalone_mode=False,\n    )\n    body = summary.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    assert \"# VN30 Reconciliation Summary\" in body\n    assert \"VHM\" in body\n    assert \"VIC\" in body\n    assert \"DUAL_SOURCE\" in body  # both stub adapters return rows\n\n\ndef test_summary_reports_dual_source_when_both_adapters_return(stub_adapters: None, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n    from apps.cli.ingest_vn30 import main\n    runner = CliRunner()\n    summary = tmp_path / \"summary.md\"\n    runner.invoke(\n        main,\n        [\n            \"--start\", \"2026-04-01\",\n            \"--end\", \"2026-04-29\",\n            \"--output\", str(tmp_path / \"vn30.sqlite\"),\n            \"--summary\", str(summary),\n            \"--rate-limit-rps\", \"1000\",\n            \"--tickers\", \"VHM\",\n        ],\n        standalone_mode=False,\n    )\n    body = summary.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    # 100000 vs 100200 = 0.2% divergence → HIGH confidence dual\n    assert \"100.0%\" in body or \"Dual-source rows: 1 (100.0%)\" in body\n\n\ndef test_invalid_ticker_subset_raises(stub_adapters: None, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n    from apps.cli.ingest_vn30 import main\n    runner = CliRunner()\n    result = runner.invoke(\n        main,\n        [\n            \"--output\", str(tmp_path / \"vn30.sqlite\"),\n            \"--tickers\", \"VHM,XXX\",  # XXX not in VN30\n        ],\n assert \"non-VN30\" in str(exc)\n\n\ndef test_default_universe_is_30_tickers() -> None:\n    from packages.domain.market_data import vn30_tickers\n    assert len(vn30_tickers()) == test_subset_run_writes_per_ticker_rows(stub_adapters: None, tmp_path: Path) -> None:","newString":"def test_subset_run_writes_per_ticker_rows(stub_adapters: None, tmp_path: Path) -> None:  # noqa: ARG001","originalFile":"\"\"\"CLI smoke tests for `apps/cli/ingest_vn30.py` (S33 Track B).\n\nNO live API calls — adapters are monkey-patched to return recorded Bar lists.\nVerifies CLI orchestration: universe resolution, per-ticker fetch + reconcile,\nSQLite write, summary markdown emission.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom datetime import UTC, date, datetime\nfrom decimal import Decimal\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\nfrom click.testing import CliRunner\n\nfrom packages.contracts import (\n    AdjustmentType,\n    Currency,\n    Money,\n    SourceProvider,\n    Ticker,\n)\nfrom packages.domain.market_data.models import Bar\n\n\ndef _bar(*, ticker: str, period_end: date, source: SourceProvider, close: str = \"100000\") -> Bar:\n    return Bar(\n        ticker=Ticker(ticker),\n        period_end=period_end,\n        filing_date=period_end,\n        ingested_at=datetime(2026, 4, 30, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC),\n        open=Money(Decimal(close), Currency.VND),\n        high=Money(Decimal(close) + Decimal(\"500\"), Currency.VND),\n        low=Money(Decimal(close) - Decimal(\"500\"), Currency.VND),\n        close=Money(Decimal(close), Currency.VND),\n        volume=1_000_000,\n        foreign_buy=0,\n        foreign_sell=0,\n        adjustment_type=AdjustmentType.BOTH,\n        source_provider=source,\n    )\n\n\n@pytest.fixture()\ndef stub_adapters(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Monkey-patch VnstockAdapter + SsiAdapter to return canned Bar lists.\"\"\"\n    from packages.infrastructure.market_data import SsiAdapter, VnstockAdapter\n\n    def vnstock_fetch(_self: object, ticker: Ticker, _start: date, _end: date) -> list[Bar]:\n        return [_bar(ticker=ticker.symbol, period_end=date(2026, 4, 28), source=SourceProvider.VNSTOCK)]\n\n    def ssi_fetch(_self: object, ticker: Ticker, _start: date, _end: date) -> list[Bar]:\n        return [_bar(ticker=ticker.symbol, period_end=date(2026, 4, 28), source=SourceProvider.SSI, close=\"100200\")]\n\n    monkeypatch.setattr(VnstockAdapter, \"fetch_daily\", vnstock_fetch)\n    monkeypatch.setattr(SsiAdapter, \"fetch_daily\", ssi_fetch)\n    monkeypatch.setattr(VnstockAdapter, \"__post_init__\", lambda _self: None)\n    monkeypatch.setattr(SsiAdapter, \"__post_init__\", lambda _self: None)\n\n\ndef test_subset_run_writes_per_ticker_rows(stub_adapters: None, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n    from apps.cli.ingest_vn30 import main\n    runner = CliRunner()\n    db = tmp_path / \"vn30.sqlite\"\n    summary = tmp_path / \"summary.md\"\n    result = runner.invoke(\n        main,\n        [\n            \"--start\", \"2026-04-01\",\n            \"--end\", \"2026-04-29\",\n            \"--output\", str(db),\n            \"--summary\", str(summary),\n            \"--rate-limit-rps\", \"1000\",\n            \"--tickers\", \"VHM,VIC,FPT\",\n        ],\n        standalone_mode=False,\n    )\n    assert result.exit_code == 0, result.output\n    assert \"vn30] OK\" in result.output\n    assert db.exists()\n    assert summary.exists()\n\n\ndef test_subset_run_emits_summary_markdown(stub_adapters: None, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n    from apps.cli.ingest_vn30 import main\n    runner = CliRunner()\n    db = tmp_path / \"vn30.sqlite\"\n    summary = tmp_path / \"summary.md\"\n    runner.invoke(\n        main,\n        [\n            \"--start\", \"2026-04-01\",\n            \"--end\", \"2026-04-29\",\n            \"--output\", str(db),\n            \"--summary\", str(summary),\n            \"--rate-limit-rps\", \"1000\",\n            \"--tickers\", \"VHM,VIC\",\n        ],\n        standalone_mode=False,\n    )\n    body = summary.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    assert \"# VN30 Reconciliation Summary\" in body\n    assert \"VHM\" in body\n    assert \"VIC\" in body\n    assert \"DUAL_SOURCE\" in body  # both stub adapters return rows\n\n\ndef test_summary_reports_dual_source_when_both_adapters_return(stub_adapters: None, tmp_path: Path) -> None:  # noqa: ARG001\n    from apps.cli.ingest_vn30 import main\n    runner = CliRunner()\n    summary = tmp_path / \"summary.md\"\n    runner.invoke(\n        main,\n        [\n            \"--start\", \"2026-04-01\",\n            \"--end\", \"2026-04-29\",\n            \"--output\", str(tmp_path / \"vn30.sqlite\"),\n            \"--summary\", str(summary),\n            \"--rate-limit-rps\", \"1000\",\n            \"--tickers\", \"VHM\",\n        ],\n        standalone_mode=False,\n    )\n    body = summary.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    # 100000 vs 100200 = 0.2% divergence → HIGH confidence dual\n    assert \"100.0%\" in body or \"Dual-source rows: 1 (100.0%)\" in body\n\n\ndef test_invalid_ticker_subset_raises(stub_adapters: None, tmp_path: Path) -> None:  # noqa: ARG001\n    from apps.cli.ingest_vn30 import main\n    runner = CliRunner()\n    result = runner.invoke(\n        main,\n        [\n            \"--output\", str(tmp_path / \"vn30.sqlite\"),\n            \"--tickers\", \"VHM,XXX\",  # XXX not in VN30\n        ],\n        standalone_mode=False,\n    )\n    # UsageError surfaces as non-zero exit + message in result.exception\n    assert result.exit_code != 0\n    exc = result.exception\n    assert exc is not None\n    assert \"non-VN30\" in str(exc)\n\n\ndef test_default_universe_is_30_tickers() -> None:\n    from packages.domain.market_data import vn30_tickers\n    assert len(vn30_tickers()) == 30\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":59,"oldLines":7,"newStart":59,"newLines":7,"lines":["     monkeypatch.setattr(SsiAdapter, \"__post_init__\", lambda _self: None)"," "," ","-def There is no SUPERVISED mode. Agents must NOT fire `AskUserQuestion` for routine handoffs (e.g., \"should I /clear?\" / \"should I continue same session?\"). Routine session-handoff is handled deterministically by existing infrastructure: Stop hook → `autonomous-stop-watchdog.sh` → Mode-A/B/C/D detection → `session-self-reboot.sh` (≥cliff) or `continue-injector.ps1` (<cliff) → fresh TUI receives \"continue\" → fresh agent reads checkpoint and resumes.\n\n**Why**: User explicit correction in S15 close 2026-04-29: \"no, autonomous is full autonomous by you, not me human-in-the-loop, or any 'supervisor' mode? its not my idea! why you have that? why q&a me to clear or continue?\" The \"SUPERVISED\" / \"AUTONOMOUS (after Track 7)\" bifurcation was fabricated by prior agents (agent-notes L-S7 / L-S8 entries treat SUPERVISED as default-until-Track-7) without user authorization. Per memory `autonomous_goals.md`: user goal #1 = reboot must be cheap; goal #2 = verify gates must FIRE deterministically not via workflow-steps; goal #3 = drift control via deterministic scripts not LLM-only.
